Experiments: 在物理移动机器人上验证了三层架构在三种异构用户界面下的互操作性，并验证了实时人机交互工作流，延迟开销可忽略不计。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人学与人机交互领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), are driving robotic architectures toward agent-based high-level orchestration, in which natural-language instructions can be translated into context-aware action sequences. While the integration of these agents and robotic capabilities is increasingly converging toward standardization through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), the upper Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) layer remains fragmented by proprietary, ad hoc interfaces that hinder real-time human-in-the-loop collaboration.
  To address this fragmentation, this paper proposes the adoption of the Agent-Client Protocol (ACP) -- a communication standard originally introduced for coding agents in software engineering -- as a unified communication contract for the HRI layer in agent-based robotic systems. By combining ACP at the interface-agent link and MCP at the agent-execution link, we formulate a fully decoupled three-layer architecture that separates human interaction, deliberative orchestration, and physical execution. This topology removes rigid architectural dependencies, enabling heterogeneous user interfaces to connect to the same robotic system and allowing the underlying robotic platform to be replaced without requiring client-specific integration changes. Moreover, it provides native support for collaborative HRI capabilities such as real-time observability, explicit human authorization, and immediate task interruption. We experimentally evaluate the proposed architecture on a physical mobile robot, demonstrating interoperability across three heterogeneous user interfaces and validating real-time human-in-the-loop workflows with negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [129] [Steering Robustness into World Action Models via Mechanistic Interpretability and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14943)
*Jihoon Hong,Julian Skifstad,Qiyue Dai,Alice Chan,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究世界动作模型(WAM)在分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题，利用机械可解释性分析WAM激活空间中的鲁棒性表征，并提出无训练干预和基于模型的最优控制干预方法(WA-LQR)。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的World Action Models鲁棒性，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人控制中的鲁棒性分析与干预；应用场景：机器人操作(涉及camera, gripper等)；传感器类型：未明确说明，涉及视觉噪声扰动；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 涉及Cosmos-Policy、DiT4DiT、LingBot-VA等世界动作模型架构；提出WA-LQR(World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator)控制器。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及鲁棒性(robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations)和steering干预效果。与遥感目标检测常用数据集(DOTA, DIOR, HRSC2016等)和指标(mAP, FPS等)无关。

Experiments: 在Cosmos-Policy和DiT4DiT模型上验证WA-LQR方法，对比无干预和prompt steering基线，在相机、夹爪和视觉噪声扰动下提升鲁棒性。消融实验涉及不同WAM架构的线性可分性对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。论文聚焦机器人控制领域的世界模型鲁棒性，属于完全不同的研究范式。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文涉及的激活空间可解释性分析和无训练干预方法属于机器人控制领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无交叉。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable semantically- and physically-informed control but are brittle under distribution shift. In this work, we use mechanistic interpretability to study how robustness-relevant perturbations are represented in WAM activation space. Comparing activations across successful and unsuccessful rollouts, we find some WAM architectures exhibit low-dimensional linear separability for robustness-critical features, while others do not. This motivates the use of contrastive activation directions for training-free WAM steering. We also show that local linearity in WAM activation dynamics enables efficient feedback steering via model-based optimal control, yielding World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR), a minimally-invasive reduced-order LQR controller. Via mechanistic evaluations, we predict strong steerability in the Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT models but weak steerability in LingBot-VA, consistent with steering intervention results. On Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT, WA-LQR generalizes contrastive directions to new tasks and improves robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations over unsteered and prompt steering baselines.

</details>


### [130] [AeroAct: Action-Centered World-Action Models for Language-Conditioned Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14997)
*Xinhong Zhang,Qiyuan Zhu,Yubo Huang,Haolin Chen,Runqing Wang,Yuhao Mo,Zhongxin Chen,Yu Hu,Xinjiang Wang,Jian Sun,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AeroAct，一个基于动作中心世界-动作模型（WAM）的四旋翼导航方法，利用预训练视频扩散Transformer预测轨迹-动作块，并在真实四旋翼上验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是四旋翼无人机（quadrotor）的语言条件导航与动作控制，属于无人机自主导航/视觉语言导航领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱图像、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言条件四旋翼导航（language-conditioned quadrotor navigation）；应用场景：无人机目标跟踪、目标搜索；传感器类型：第一人称视角相机（egocentric camera）；目标类别：摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频扩散Transformer（video diffusion Transformer），预测局部轨迹-动作块（local trajectory-action chunks），输入为第一人称视觉历史、本体感知（proprioception）和语言指令。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到在物理四旋翼（physical quadrotor）上执行，但未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标；实验包括闭环仿真（closed-loop simulation）和真实世界实验（real-world experiments）。

Experiments: 实验表明时间视觉上下文（temporal visual context）改善了目标跟踪和目标搜索性能，WAM策略可在物理四旋翼上执行。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署优化；3. 未报告计算资源消耗指标；4. 方法针对低空无人机导航，非遥感卫星场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：视频扩散Transformer用于预测未来帧作为监督信号的思想，或许可迁移到遥感视频目标检测中的时序建模，但与本课题核心方向（遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化）关联度很低。

Abstract: Language-conditioned quadrotor flight requires a policy to ground semantic goals, anticipate the visual consequences of ego-motion, and output control references that remain smooth and dynamically executable under rapidly changing first-person views. Existing aerial vision-language navigation and vision-language-action methods commonly use discrete actions, high-level waypoints, or instantaneous velocity commands, which provide limited supervision about how flight actions change future observations. We present AeroAct, an action-centered world-action model (WAM) for quadrotor navigation. To the best of our knowledge, AeroAct is the first WAM instantiated and demonstrated for real-world aerial flight. The model adapts a pretrained video diffusion Transformer to predict local trajectory-action chunks from egocentric visual history, proprioception, and language. Future first-person frames are used during training as dense consequence supervision, while deployment directly decodes actions without generating future video. To obtain aligned visual, state, language, and dynamically feasible action data, we build a DiffAero-based pipeline with complementary Isaac Lab and 3D Gaussian splatting renderers. We further introduce a low-cost handheld collection device that couples camera observations with motion estimates to recreate flight-like egocentric trajectories, and a self-guidance procedure that improves temporal consistency across overlapping trajectory chunks. Closed-loop simulation and real-world experiments show that temporal visual context improves target tracking and object-search performance, and that WAM-based policies can be executed on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [131] [CosFly-VLA: A Spatially Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15004)
*Ruilong Ren,Songsheng Cheng,Yunpeng Zhou,Hanxuan Chen,Xiangyue Wang,Tianle Zeng,Shuai Yuan,Binbo Li,Hanzhong Guo,Ji Pei,Da Zhang,Kangli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CosFly-VLA，一种面向无人机城市动态目标跟踪的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过空间感知预训练、课程微调和闭环强化学习，解决遮挡下的目标丢失问题。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及无人机（UAV）视角下的目标跟踪，与遥感/卫星有一定场景关联，但核心是视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，面向的是低空无人机连续跟踪任务，而非遥感卫星在轨目标检测。不涉及遥感图像（SAR/多光谱/高光谱）、不涉及目标检测（是跟踪任务）、不涉及任何轻量化方法（模型0.8B参数量，属于大模型范畴）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：动态目标跟踪（dynamic target tracking）；应用场景：无人机城市环境；传感器类型：可见光（UAV-view RGB）；目标类别：通用目标（未明确指定具体类别）。

Model architecture: CosFly-VLA，基于VLA（Vision-Language-Action）架构，包含空间感知预训练（Spatially Grounded CPT）、多阶段课程SFT、CoT推理训练、闭环RL优化。模型规模0.8B参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。模型参数量0.8B，属于大模型范畴，与轻量化方向相反。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及模型规模为0.8B参数，对于星上/边缘部署而言过大。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：500k混合池用于CPT预训练；自然/长遮挡数据用于课程SFT。评价指标：开环ADE（Average Displacement Error）、闭环SR（Success Rate）。

Experiments: 与OpenVLA对比：CosFly-VLA-0.8B在seen-test上ADE降低34.1%，unseen-test上降低35.3%；闭环优化使SR分别提升29.8%和2.5%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 模型参数量0.8B，远大于星上可部署的轻量模型；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 任务为跟踪而非检测；5. 未报告推理速度、计算量等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1. 课程学习（Curriculum Learning）策略可用于遥感目标检测训练数据组织；2. 闭环优化思想可用于在轨检测模型的在线自适应更新；3. 空间感知预训练（深度/距离/3D推理）的思路可迁移到遥感多模态融合。但整体方向差异较大。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking is essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in complex urban environments, where both the target and the camera viewpoint change continuously. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can track visible targets effectively, but their performance often degrades when buildings, vegetation, or roadside objects block the line of sight. During sustained occlusion, a policy may lose the target state, execute actions toward an incorrect region, and amplify this error through subsequent observations until re-acquisition becomes impossible. To this end, we present CosFly-VLA, a spatially aware VLA model that jointly grounds the target, estimates its visibility, and generates continuous flight actions through a structured prediction interface. To train this policy, we use a large-scale recipe over diverse data sources. Spatially Grounded Continued Pretraining (CPT) on a 500k mixed pool injects UAV-view depth, distance, and 3-D spatial reasoning. A three-stage Curriculum-based Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) process then specializes the tracker through multi-head warm-up followed by two-stage curriculum learning over natural and hard / long-occlusion data. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training subsequently teaches recovery-oriented reasoning traces before structured answers. Finally, a closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage optimizes tracking behavior with a multi-component reward covering stand-off tracking, grounding quality, collision avoidance, and task success. Relative to OpenVLA, CosFly-VLA-0.8B reduces open-loop Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 34.1% on seen-test and 35.3% on unseen-test. Closed-loop optimization improves Success Rate (SR) by 29.8% and 2.5%, respectively. These results demonstrate progress from visible-frame imitation toward spatially grounded action-closed-loop control, evaluated under a shared oracle state history.

</details>


### [132] [Risk-Aware Belief Control Barrier Functions over Random Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15016)
*Shaohang Han,Gang Chen,Yixi Cai,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius,Patric Jensfelt,Javier Alonso-Mora,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于风险感知信念控制障碍函数(BCBF)的框架，用于在未知动态环境中保障机器人安全，通过随机有限集(RFS)信念和SMC-PHD滤波器处理多目标状态不确定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人安全控制（尤其是水下机器人），涉及多目标状态估计与避障，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测模型或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人安全控制、多目标状态估计、避障；场景：未知动态环境（水下实验）；传感器类型：摘要未明确说明（可能为声纳或视觉传感器）；目标类别：动态障碍物/移动物体。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱等。

Model architecture: 核心框架为风险感知信念控制障碍函数(BCBF)，底层使用SMC-PHD滤波器（序贯蒙特卡洛概率假设密度滤波器）进行多目标信念状态估计，以粒子集表示不确定性。不涉及任何深度学习目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量化网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境+真实水下实验。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，主要验证安全控制的有效性和效率。

Experiments: 仿真实验+真实水下机器人实验，验证了所提BCBF框架的有效性和效率。摘要未提供与基线方法的定量对比或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型设计、压缩方法或部署策略。该论文属于机器人控制/安全领域，而非计算机视觉或遥感智能处理领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety in unknown, dynamic environments is a fundamental requirement. It involves inferring the states of an unknown and time-varying number of moving objects from noisy, incomplete measurements. We address safe control under the induced multi-object state uncertainty with a risk-aware belief control barrier function (BCBF) framework. The uncertainty is captured by a random finite set (RFS) belief, estimated by a sequential Monte Carlo probability hypothesis density (SMC-PHD) filter that represents it with a set of particles. Building directly on these particles, we construct a nonsmooth BCBF, establish forward invariance of the safe set under continuous prediction, and derive an explicit condition under which discrete updates preserve safety. Simulation and real-world underwater experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Agile Navigation in Crowded Environments for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15036)
*Shuyu Wu,Zeyu Liu,Tianbao Zhang,Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Mingkang Xiong,Wei Xi,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VOP-Nav，一种结合速度障碍法几何安全性与端到端学习鲁棒性的四足机器人导航系统，利用多帧LiDAR数据隐式编码动态约束并预测安全速度区域。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。研究对象是四足机器人导航，传感器为LiDAR，应用场景为地面动态拥挤环境，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关要素，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 四足机器人导航与避障；地面动态拥挤环境；传感器为LiDAR；不涉及遥感目标检测任务。

Model architecture: VOP-Net，处理多帧LiDAR数据，隐式编码动态约束并预测安全速度区域。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行了真实部署验证，但摘要未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: Isaac Gym仿真环境评估；真实环境在Unitree Go2上验证。评价指标：成功率、 locomotion speed、碰撞避免能力。未使用任何遥感标准数据集。

Experiments: 在Isaac Gym中VOP-Nav成功率高于所有基线方法，平衡了运动速度与碰撞避免。真实场景部署验证了鲁棒性和效率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术要素。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：VO理论中"将安全约束同时作为推理输入和训练奖励信号"的双重利用思想，在遥感在轨场景中可类比为"将物理约束（如星上计算资源限制、实时性要求）同时融入模型推理和训练损失函数"，但这一思路过于泛化，且与遥感目标检测轻量化的核心问题（模型压缩、加速、部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Navigating dynamic and crowded environments presents significant challenges for quadruped robots due to severe sensor occlusion and unpredictable human motion. Existing approaches face a trade-off: model-based methods, such as Velocity Obstacles (VO), theoretically guarantee safety but rely on accurate obstacle motion estimates that often fail in dense crowds, while end-to-end learning methods offer robustness but lack motion prediction capability of obstacles, leading to collisions or conservative behaviors. To solve this, we propose VOP-Nav, a novel navigation system that combines the geometric safety of VO with the agile adaptability of end-to-end learning. Using only local onboard observations, our system avoids explicit obstacle detection and tracking pipelines. The VOP-Net processes multi-frame LiDAR data to implicitly encode dynamic constraints and predict a safe velocity region derived from Velocity Obstacle theory. Importantly, the VO predictions serve a dual role: they are used as input to the navigation policy during inference and as a reward signal during training to encourage safe motion. Evaluations in Isaac Gym demonstrate that VOP-Nav achieves higher success rates than all baselines while balancing locomotion speed and collision avoidance. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates the system's robustness and efficiency in complex indoor and outdoor dynamic environments.

</details>


### [134] [DriftWorld: Fast World Modeling through Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15065)
*Susie Lu,Haonan Chen,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DriftWorld，一种基于漂移生成模型的动作条件世界模型，通过单次前向传播替代扩散模型的多步迭代去噪，实现30+ fps的快速未来帧生成，用于机器人规划与控制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向机器人操控（robotic manipulation）场景，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关任务；不涉及目标检测；不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然其核心思想是加速生成式模型的推理速度，但应用场景和任务类型与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人世界模型（world model）用于动作规划与控制；应用场景：视觉机器人操控（vision-based robotic manipulation）；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：机器人操作对象（如推块、抓取等）。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: DriftWorld基于漂移生成模型（drifting generative models），学习动作条件漂移（action-conditioned drift），在训练阶段完成漂移学习，推理时从当前观测和候选动作序列通过单次前向传播生成未来帧，无需迭代去噪。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。其加速策略是通过改变生成范式（从扩散模型的迭代去噪变为单次前向传播）实现推理加速，属于算法层面的效率提升，而非模型压缩或轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到推理速度30+ fps，比扩散基线平均快17倍。但该速度是在机器人操控场景下测得，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, Robomimic；评价指标：决策性能（decision-making performance）、推理时间（inference time）、rollout准确率、与真实策略的相关系数（correlation with ground truth up to 0.99）。

Experiments: 在多个机器人操控基准上，DriftWorld以远少于扩散世界模型的推理时间实现了最先进的决策性能；作为离线模拟器，rollout得分与真实策略相关性高达0.99。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 仅针对机器人操控场景验证，未在遥感图像上测试；3. 未涉及目标检测任务；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；5. 未讨论星上/边缘部署的硬件约束。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但其"将迭代推理变为单次前向传播"的思想有一定启发性：在遥感在轨目标检测中，如果检测模型依赖某种迭代优化过程（如DETR类模型的解码器迭代、或扩散检测器），可借鉴类似思路将迭代过程压缩为单次前向传播以加速推理。但总体而言，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15111)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无信号灯路口的分布式ISAC车辆协同框架，利用目标导向语义通信和MHPPO强化学习联合优化感知与通信传输决策，以减少信令开销并实现100%无碰撞车辆协调。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于地面车辆路口的分布式感知-通信一体化（ISAC）协同控制，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等压缩加速方法。属于智能交通系统（ITS）与通信交叉领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无信号灯路口的车辆协同控制（vehicle coordination），包括车辆状态感知与指挥控制信号传输。场景：地面交通路口，多RSU协同，中心BS管理。传感器类型：ISAC感知信号（非光学/SAR/遥感传感器）。目标类别：车辆（vehicle），非遥感目标检测中的舰船/飞机/车辆。

Model architecture: 核心框架：Goal-oriented Semantic Communication (GSC) 框架；EKF（扩展卡尔曼滤波）用于车辆状态预测与分布式感知融合；Masked Hybrid PPO (MHPPO) 强化学习框架用于联合决策感知传输、C&C传输及信号内容；Uncertainty-aware Transmission Design (UTD) 包含鲁棒波束赋形与基于VoI的时分功率分配。不涉及任何视觉目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理帧率等部署指标。论文关注的是通信信令开销（signaling overhead）的降低，而非模型计算开销的优化。不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘/星上部署硬件。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：碰撞率（collision-free rate，达到100%）、信令开销（signaling overhead）。对比基线：predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art相关研究及多个消融基线。

Experiments: 仿真实验（simulation results）。主要结果：所提框架实现100%无碰撞车辆协调，同时显著降低信令开销。未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。2. 仅基于仿真验证，未涉及真实道路测试或遥感数据验证。3. 未讨论模型计算复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标。4. 未涉及任何视觉感知模型（目标检测、分割等），仅使用EKF进行状态估计。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：论文中"goal-oriented"语义通信的思想——只传输语义重要的信息以减少冗余——在遥感星上场景中，可类比为"只回传检测结果中置信度高/语义重要的目标，而非全图"，但这属于通信传输层面的优化，而非模型轻量化本身。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15129)
*Jonathan Rainer Lippert,Kai Ploeger,Abir Chowdhury,Hermann Müller,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协作抛接球（partner juggling）的实时规划与控制架构，实现机器人与人类在多人抛接球模式下的同步协调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是人机物理交互中的动态物体交换（抛接球），属于机器人控制与运动规划领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人机协作抛接球（human-robot partner juggling）；应用场景：物理人机交互；传感器类型：视觉追踪（predictive ball tracking）；目标类别：球（ball）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 实时规划与控制架构，包含：predictive ball tracking（预测性球追踪）、adaptive online trajectory optimization（自适应在线轨迹优化，使用multiple-shooting formulation）、state-machine-based coordination logic（基于状态机的协调逻辑）。不涉及任何视觉检测模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"real-time"实时性，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 用户研究（user study）：8名参与者，技能从新手到专家不等。评价指标：连续接球次数（consecutive catches）、成功率（success rate）。不涉及任何遥感数据集或标准检测指标（mAP, FPS等）。

Experiments: 所有参与者在10分钟测试中均超过此前报道的最佳结果；一名参与者将三球级联抛接记录提升5倍至20次连续接球；另一名参与者在单球接发场景中实现40次连续接球、100%成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测模型或轻量化技术。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling

</details>


### [137] [Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15156)
*Aniol Civit,Antonio Andriella,Alba Martínez,Joan Ars,Aida Ribera,Cristian Barrué,Guillem Alenyà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于行为树架构的社交机器人框架，通过视觉骨架追踪引导老年人完成标准化衰弱评估测试（SPPB、TUG），并与临床参考仪器进行对比验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医疗康复场景中的老年人衰弱评估，使用社交机器人和视觉骨架追踪，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：老年人衰弱评估与跌倒风险评估；应用场景：康复中心/医疗保健；传感器类型：RGB摄像头（视觉骨架追踪）、惯性测量单元（IMU）、步态分析走道；目标类别：人体骨架关键点、步态参数。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 行为树（Behaviour Tree）架构，协调感知、决策、交互和测量模块。视觉部分使用基于视觉的骨架追踪（vision-based skeleton tracking）。摘要未说明具体骨架检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：在康复中心研究实验室6个月内采集，N=81名老年人；评价指标：组内相关系数（ICC）、Cohen's Kappa系数（k）；临床测试：SPPB（Short Physical Performance Battery）、TUG（Timed Up and Go）。

Experiments: 机器人测量与治疗师评估及临床参考仪器（步态分析走道、IMU）对比。大多数测试完成时间和步态相关参数ICC>0.9；SPPB总分机器人与治疗师k=0.67（substantial agreement），机器人与IMU k=0.55（moderate agreement）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。摘要未说明骨架追踪模型的参数量、推理速度、是否可部署在边缘设备等。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医疗机器人/人机交互领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults. Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15163)
*Weishuai Zeng,Kangning Yin,Xiaojie Niu,Shunlin Lu,Weixiang Zhong,Jiahe Chen,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Zirui Wang,Furui Xu,Ming Zhou,Kailin Li,Weinan Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向人形机器人控制的Behavior Foundation Model (BFM)，通过运动跟踪学习范式、策略数据与参考动作多样性的协同、以及Humanoid Transformer架构实现可扩展的人形控制，在仿真和真实部署中显著降低了关键点位置误差。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于人形机器人控制（humanoid control），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然提到了"real-time responses"和"deployment"，但语境是人形机器人实时控制，而非星上/边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为人形机器人控制，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人全身协调控制（whole-body coordination）；应用场景：仿真环境与真实人形机器人部署；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：人形机器人姿态/运动，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: Humanoid Transformer，一种可扩展的模型架构，促进结构化行为表征的自然涌现。具体结构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关指标。仅提到"real-time responses"和"real-world deployment"，但未给出具体量化数据，且部署场景为人形机器人而非卫星/边缘设备。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE)，在测试集上local mode降低10%以上，global mode降低82%。

Experiments: 在仿真和真实世界部署中进行了实验，相比已有的人形控制器，在控制精度和任务泛化方面有显著提升。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星领域；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法；4. 部署相关量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）完全缺失；5. 研究场景（人形机器人）与在轨遥感目标检测差异极大，方法难以直接迁移。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：论文强调"learning paradigm"、"data diversity"和"model architecture"三者的协同设计（coordination）对性能提升的重要性，这种系统化设计思路在遥感轻量化检测模型的设计中也可借鉴——即不能只关注模型压缩本身，还需考虑训练范式、数据策略与网络结构的联合优化。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向基本无关。

Abstract: Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.

</details>


### [139] [AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15172)
*Seok Joon Kim,Junho Lee,Federica Spinola,Taein Kwon,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AHEAD，一种基于VR的遥操作机器人系统，通过注意力分类器预测操作者意图（抓取物体和放置槽位），减少机器人反应延迟并降低操作者负担。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是VR遥操作机器人系统（teleoperation），涉及人机交互、意图预测、机器人控制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：VR遥操作机器人抓取与放置（pick-and-place）；应用场景：机器人遥操作、人机交互；传感器类型：3D手部/头部信号（VR设备）；目标类别：抓取物体和放置槽位。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于注意力的分类器（attention-based classifier），处理3D手部/头部信号和场景上下文，预测意图。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、实时推理等部署相关信息。仅提到机器人反应延迟降低0.6s（物体）和1.4s（槽位），但这属于系统级延迟而非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 用户实验（user study），非公开遥感数据集。评价指标：Top1准确率（抓取物体76%，放置槽位76%）、机器人反应延迟（latency）、操作者负荷（operator load）。

Experiments: 意图预测模块Top1准确率：抓取物体76%，放置槽位76%；用户实验显示AHEAD相比基线减少机器人反应延迟0.6s（物体）和1.4s（槽位）；操作者报告更低负荷。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、是否可部署于资源受限平台。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人遥操作领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、传感器类型、部署约束、技术方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.

</details>


### [140] [RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15275)
*Yunfan Jiang,Yevgen Chebotar,Ruijie Zheng,Fengyuan Hu,Yunhao Ge,Jimmy Wu,Tianyuan Dai,Scott Reed,Li Fei-Fei,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboTTT，一种将机器人策略的视觉运动上下文扩展到8000时间步（比现有方法高三个数量级）的测试时训练方法，用于机器人操作任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操作（robot manipulation）领域的视觉-语言-动作策略，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作策略（robot manipulation）；应用场景：真实机器人操作任务（real-robot manipulation tasks）；传感器类型：视觉传感器（visuomotor context）；目标类别：机器人操作物体。完全不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 策略的机器人基础模型，集成Test-Time Training (TTT)，使用梯度下降更新参数作为循环状态（fast weights），结合sequence action forcing与truncated backpropagation through time进行长序列训练。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。论文核心是扩展上下文长度而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"without growing inference latency"，但未给出具体数值，且针对的是机器人场景而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实机器人操作任务（real-robot manipulation tasks），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标：任务完成率（overall performance improvement by 87%），五阶段十步组装任务完成情况。

Experiments: 在真实机器人操作任务上，RoboTTT相比单步上下文基线提升87%性能；8K时间步上下文训练比1K时间步上下文训练提升62%；完整完成5分钟十阶段组装任务（基线从未完成）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。论文未涉及遥感数据、目标检测任务、轻量化方法或星上部署约束。其核心贡献（长上下文测试时训练）在机器人领域有意义，但对遥感目标检测轻量化课题无直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的技术交叉点是：Test-Time Training（测试时训练）作为一种自适应机制，理论上可被用于遥感在轨模型对数据分布漂移（如光照变化、传感器退化）的在线自适应，但论文本身未涉及轻量化约束，且机器人领域的TTT计算开销在星上资源受限条件下可能不可行。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/

</details>

### [141] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于可解释AI（XAI）的立场论文，呼吁从临时性解释方法转向以人为中心、可行动化的反馈驱动系统，与遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署等任何与本研究方向相关的内容。论文主题是Explainable AI (XAI)的方法论反思，属于纯机器学习/可解释性领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体的视觉任务、应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型结构或网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题完全无关，没有任何可借鉴的技术内容。论文讨论的是XAI领域的方法论问题，属于不同的研究方向。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与本研究方向完全无关，不提供任何可复现的技术方法或可改进方向。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [142] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种类别感知的零样本提示重加权方法（CARPRT），通过无训练方式为每个类别自适应调整多个prompt的权重，提升VLM零样本分类性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的零样本分类中的prompt集成策略优化，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署。虽然摘要中出现了"an aerial view of"作为prompt示例，但仅作为说明性例子，并非以遥感场景为核心研究对象。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：零样本图像分类（zero-shot image classification）；应用场景：通用图像分类；传感器类型：通用RGB图像；目标类别：通用物体类别（如cat, airport, apple等）。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM），如CLIP类模型。核心改进是提出类别感知的prompt重加权机制（CARPRT），为每个类别独立计算prompt权重，而非所有类别共享同一权重向量。方法为training-free，无需额外训练。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。方法本身是training-free的，但依赖大规模预训练VLM（如CLIP），推理开销较大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：标准图像分类benchmarks（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：零样本分类准确率（zero-shot classification accuracy）。

Experiments: 在标准图像分类benchmarks上，CARPRT优于现有的class-independent reweighting方法，验证了建模prompt-类别依赖关系对零样本预测的有效性。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 依赖大规模预训练VLM，推理计算量大；3. 仅在通用图像分类上验证，未在遥感场景下测试；4. 未讨论实时性或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：如果未来研究遥感场景下的VLM零样本检测（如遥感目标检测中的prompt工程），该方法的类别感知重加权思想可迁移到遥感领域，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（轻量化网络设计、剪枝量化蒸馏、边缘部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [143] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LightGBM的机器学习框架，利用开放地理空间数据预测代表性杂波高度（RCH），用于无线电传播和干扰分析，替代ITU-R P.452-18中基于土地利用类别的固定杂波高度方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然论文使用了光学遥感产品作为特征输入，但其核心任务是预测无线电传播中的杂波高度（RCH），属于无线电传播和频谱协调领域，而非遥感目标检测。不涉及目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归预测（RCH预测）；应用场景：无线电传播分析、低地球轨道地面站选址、频谱协调；传感器类型：LiDAR（用于生成训练标签）、光学遥感产品（作为特征）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: LightGBM梯度提升树回归模型，非深度学习神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。模型为LightGBM树模型，未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然提到LightGBM具有"efficiency"优势，但未给出具体数值，且该模型面向地面部署而非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：美国地质调查局3D高程计划LiDAR数据（生成标签）、全球土地覆盖、地形、人口、热红外和光学遥感产品（特征）；评价指标：MAE=1.79m, R²=0.765，以及米级误差、容忍带精度、过/低估尾部、与ITU杂波高度一致性、SHAP可解释性分析。

Experiments: LightGBM相比ITU基线方法绝对误差降低60%以上；进行了分割特征消融、非森林消融、土地覆盖匹配的国际验证；SHAP分析显示树冠覆盖、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率是最重要预测因子。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关；2. 模型为传统机器学习（树模型），非深度学习目标检测模型；3. 仅使用美国LiDAR数据训练，国际验证有限；4. 未涉及任何在轨部署或边缘部署相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点：论文展示了如何利用开放地理空间遥感数据（如土地覆盖、光谱反射率）作为特征，这对遥感特征工程有一定启发，但与目标检测模型轻量化的核心课题无关。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [144] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C3R，一个即插即用的控制层，用于跨域检索时按域认证污染预算，在无法保证时选择弃权而非静默违规。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是跨域检索中的域污染控制与认证问题，属于信息检索/自然语言处理领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：跨域检索中的域污染控制与认证；应用场景：多域混合语料库检索（如联邦法规等文本数据）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: C3R是一个drop-in控制层，基于风险控制预测集的两拆分方案，与reranker无关，冻结堆栈。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：开放测试平台（包括联邦法规公共数据集）；评价指标：域污染预算、召回率、认证违规率、LLM下游探测的grounding质量。

Experiments: 在千次重采样校准中，认证从未违规（稳定性结果），而边际控制在每次采样中都在污染最严重的域违规；软降级方法在同等认证污染下比最强校准级联保留更多召回率。方法在多个开放测试平台上复现。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术点。

Ideas for my research: 无。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无关，不提供可借鉴的技术思路。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [145] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将变分量子电路注入U-Net瓶颈层（QuFeX和QB-Net两种量子电路结构），用于卫星图像野火检测的语义分割任务，在Sen2Fire数据集上量子混合模型F1略优于经典U-Net基线。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文使用卫星遥感图像（Sen2Fire数据集），属于遥感/Earth observation场景，但任务为语义分割（wildfire detection），非目标检测；且核心贡献是量子-经典混合方法，不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等任何轻量化技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向仅遥感场景相关，但任务类型（分割vs检测）和方法取向（量子vs轻量化）均不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分 — 与课题方向弱相关，阅读优先级低。理由：任务为语义分割非目标检测，方法为量子混合非轻量化，且无任何部署效率指标。除非对量子遥感方法或Sen2Fire数据集有特殊兴趣，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语义分割（semantic image segmentation），具体为野火检测（wildfire detection）；传感器类型：卫星光学遥感（Sen2Fire数据集，推测为Sentinel-2多光谱影像）；目标类别：野火区域（二值分割）；场景：遥感卫星图像。

Model architecture: 基础架构：U-Net；创新点：在U-Net瓶颈层注入变分量子电路（variational quantum circuit），具体测试了QuFeX和QB-Net两种量子电路结构（ansatzes）；另对比了经典FPN（Feature Pyramid Network）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到"compression of parameters"（参数压缩）但未说明具体压缩技术（如剪枝、量化等），且该压缩是作为经典改进的一部分，并非论文核心贡献。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何主流轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。量子-经典混合模型在星上部署的可行性存疑，因为变分量子电路需要量子计算硬件支持，目前不适用于在轨边缘设备（FPGA/NPU等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Sen2Fire（主数据集，卫星遥感野火数据集）、CaBuAr（California Burned Areas，跨数据集迁移验证）；评价指标：F1 score（F1分数）。

Experiments: 主要结果：QB-Net F1=31.18，QuFeX F1=30.79，经典U-Net基线F1=28.71，经典FPN F1=31.13；数据混合（data mixing）消除地理域偏移后，FPN F1提升至39.76；在CaBuAr数据集上进行了跨数据集迁移验证。

Limitations: 1. 任务为语义分割而非目标检测，与课题方向任务类型不匹配；2. 量子-经典混合方法需要量子硬件支持，在轨部署可行性极低；3. 未提供任何模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. F1分数整体偏低（30-40区间），模型精度有限；5. 量子方法的实际优势微弱（相比经典FPN提升不到1个F1点），且数据混合带来的提升远大于量子方法本身。

Ideas for my research: 1. 可借鉴Sen2Fire和CaBuAr数据集作为遥感分割/检测的补充验证数据；2. 数据混合（data mixing）消除地理域偏移的思路可用于遥感目标检测中的域适应问题；3. 量子方法本身对轻量化课题借鉴意义有限，但可关注量子启发式特征提取方法是否可转化为轻量模块；4. 论文中提到的FPN架构在遥感分割中表现良好，可考虑将FPN用于遥感目标检测的轻量化设计。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [146] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向可执行沙箱环境的强化学习算法BPO，利用沙箱的可快照、可恢复特性，通过分支轨迹拓扑降低方差，提升LLM智能体训练效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于LLM智能体在可执行沙箱环境中的强化学习算法改进，属于NLP/RL领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM智能体强化学习训练（WebShop、ALFWorld、SWE-bench）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B backbone的LLM智能体，算法层面提出BPO（Branching Policy Optimization），核心是分支轨迹拓扑结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WebShop、ALFWorld、SWE-bench Verified；指标：成功率（success rate）、梯度范数方差（gradient-norm variance）、策略更新次数。

Experiments: 在三个基准上，BPO相比GRPO和RLOO提升3.6-6.1个绝对百分点的成功率，梯度范数方差减半，使用38%更少的策略更新即可达到最佳基线性能。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM+RL领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [147] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PiVoT，一种基于变分推理的贝叶斯多目标检测与跟踪方法，用于噪声雷达点云，无需训练即可实现端到端检测跟踪，在汽车雷达数据集上达到实时性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于汽车雷达（automotive radar）点云的多目标检测与跟踪，属于地面车载感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也未涉及星上部署或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多目标检测与跟踪（multi-object detection and tracking）；场景：汽车雷达（automotive radar）点云；传感器：雷达（radar），非遥感卫星传感器；目标类别：摘要未说明具体目标类别，泛指车辆等地面物体。

Model architecture: PiVoT基于贝叶斯框架和泊松测量模型（Poisson measurement model），采用变分推理（variational inference）进行联合推断，包括目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率。关键创新包括：birth pruning、二次到线性复杂度缩减、高效Doppler泊松模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。虽然论文提到"quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions"和"computationally efficient"等计算效率优化，但这些属于贝叶斯推理的算法加速，并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets"，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。且该实时性针对的是汽车雷达场景，非遥感卫星在轨部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：现代汽车雷达数据集（full-scale modern automotive radar datasets），非遥感数据集；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提到与deep-learning detection benchmark对比性能。

Experiments: 实验表明PiVoT在挑战性场景下显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，可扩展到上千个目标，对视觉上不可区分的杂波具有鲁棒性，在汽车雷达数据集上达到实时性能，性能与深度学习检测基准相当。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向汽车雷达场景，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；3. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标；4. 方法基于贝叶斯推理框架，与主流深度学习检测器范式差异较大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1. 其"quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions"思路可用于降低遥感检测模型的计算复杂度；2. 其无需训练（training-free）的特性在星上资源受限场景下有一定启发性，但贝叶斯方法与深度学习检测器路线差异较大，直接迁移困难。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [148] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究金融情感提取，使用10-K文件的风险披露部分，结合回报和波动率标签进行监督式情感词典学习，与遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于金融自然语言处理（NLP）领域，研究10-K文件的情感提取与市场结果的相关性。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融情感提取（sentiment extraction）；应用场景：金融风险披露文本分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构，仅提及使用监督式词典学习方法（supervised lexicon-learning approach）和Loughran-McDonald词典基线。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1,383份10-K文件，来自94家Nasdaq-100科技公司（2006-2023）；评价指标：分类准确率（classification accuracy）、与市场结果的相关系数（correlation with realised market outcomes）、定性词汇内容分析（qualitative lexical content）。

Experiments: 在三个聚合层级（行业、投资组合、单个公司）上，分别使用回报标签和波动率标签训练情感分数，共生成12个情感指标。实验发现：全文文本在行业和投资组合层级表现更好，而Item 1A部分在单个公司层级表现更优。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级上与价格呈强负相关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴的技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于金融NLP领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术方法、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text. These findings, and the design choices they motivate, establish the sentiment-generation methodology underlying a subsequent, larger-scale, multi-source system.

</details>


### [149] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数字孪生启发的时序生存预测框架，用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者的功能衰退预测和辅助设备使用时间预测，属于生物医学/临床决策支持领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全聚焦于生物医学领域（ALS疾病预测），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关概念，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：疾病进展预测、生存分析、辅助设备使用时间预测；应用场景：临床决策支持、精准医学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件（TTE）模型；广义加性混合模型（GAMM）；Cox比例风险模型；时序机器学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该工作面向临床环境，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：整合的ALS纵向数据集（包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动能力、人口统计信息）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（如C-index、AUC等未提及）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果数值、对比方法或消融实验。仅提到Cox模型识别出下肢功能（尤其是行走和爬楼梯）是早期使用轮椅的最强预测因子。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化技术信息。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。数字孪生（digital twin）概念本身在遥感领域有应用潜力（如卫星数字孪生），但本文的生物医学实现方式与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向差距过大，不建议作为参考。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [150] [Low-Latency Relay Selection in NR-V2X Vehicular Communications via Graph Isomorphism Networks with Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14176)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini,Pierpaolo Salvo,Paola Vocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图神经网络（GINE）的边缘感知学习优化框架，用于C-V2X网络中实时中继选择，并引入GINE剪枝MILP的混合策略（GP-MILP）以平衡最优性与实时性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于C-V2X（蜂窝车联网）通信中的中继选择问题，属于无线通信/网络优化领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然使用了"剪枝"（pruning）一词，但指的是用GINE预测剪枝MILP搜索空间，与模型轻量化中的剪枝（network pruning）完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：C-V2X网络中继选择（relay selection）；场景：密集城市环境车联网；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE)，一种图神经网络架构，用于处理V2X快照构建的有向图，节点特征编码车辆状态和流量需求，边特征编码无线链路容量。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明模型轻量化方法。文中"pruning"指用GINE预测结果剪枝MILP的搜索空间（优化求解加速），而非神经网络剪枝。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。但报告了推理延迟：所有实例在5ms内完成；GP-MILP混合策略在98%以上的图实例中求解器运行时间低于30ms。未提及参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：通过OSM-SUMO-GEMV²管道生成的大规模数据集；评价指标：链路级准确率（accuracy 0.9589）、F1-score（0.9544）、端到端连接增益（up to 9.2% with four RSUs, 12% with two RSUs）、推理延迟（<5ms）、求解器运行时间（<30ms for >98% instances）。

Experiments: GINE在链路级决策上接近MILP最优解（准确率0.9589，F1-score 0.9544）；相比1跳MILP基线，端到端连接增益最高达12%；GP-MILP保持与MILP等价的目标值，同时满足NR-V2X延迟预算。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型泛化性、不同场景下的鲁棒性等。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：(1) Learning-to-Optimize范式：用神经网络逼近传统优化算法（如MILP）的决策，可用于遥感星上资源调度或模型选择；(2) GINE处理图结构数据的思想，可用于遥感场景中目标关系建模（如舰船编队检测）；(3) 混合推理策略（神经网络+精确优化）的思路，可用于星上轻量模型+地面精细模型的协同推理。但整体而言，该论文与遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency uplink connectivity is a key requirement for C-V2X networks in dense urban environments, where fast channel variations and blockages often degrade direct vehicle-to-infrastructure links. Multi-hop relaying can restore coverage, but relay-link activation under radio, capacity, and routing constraints results in an NP-hard optimisation problem, typically solved via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), whose runtime scales poorly with graph size. This paper introduces an edge-aware Learning-to-Optimise framework for real-time relay selection. Each V2X snapshot is modelled as a directed graph: node features encode vehicle state and traffic demand, while edge features capture radio-link capacity. An offline MILP oracle generates optimal relay configurations that supervise a Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE), enabling edge-level relay activation through a single forward pass, with tightly bounded inference latency. To bridge learning and exact optimisation, we also propose a hybrid GINE-Pruned MILP (GP-MILP) strategy in which GINE predictions prune the MILP search space. Experiments on a large-scale dataset generated via an OSM-SUMO-GEMV$^2$ pipeline show that GINE closely matches MILP decisions at the link level (accuracy 0.9589), F1-score (0.9544) on validation) and yields consistent end-to-end connectivity gains over a 1-hop MILP baseline (up to 9.2% with four RSUs and 12% with two RSUs). Inference latency remains tightly bounded, with all evaluated instances completing within 5~ms. Moreover, GP-MILP preserves MILP-equivalent solutions (same objective value) while achieving solver runtimes below 30~ms for more than 98%) of the graph instances, making MILP-grade optimisation compatible with stringent NR-V2X latency budgets.

</details>


### [151] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型(LLM)服务中监督微调(SFT)与上下文学习(ICL)两种个性化方法之间的统计-经济权衡，分析用户选择、资源拥塞和平台策略问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦LLM服务中的个性化算法选择(SFT vs ICL)和资源拥塞经济学问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM个性化服务算法选择；应用场景：AI平台服务；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用GPT-2进行实验验证。

Lightweight method: 论文将ICL视为"轻量级"个性化方法（相对于SFT），但这里的"轻量"是指计算资源消耗层面的相对概念，并非模型压缩/加速领域的轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是云端LLM服务场景，而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验使用GPT-2在线性回归任务上验证理论预测；此外对21个主要AI平台的文档进行了调研。

Experiments: 主要实验结果：ICL和SFT在不同条件下各有优势，拥塞会改变两者的排序；资源消耗呈现非单调性；提供两种个性化方法不会损害平台最大利润。平台调研显示同时提供SFT和ICL的平台比例从2021年的9.5%增长到2025年的71.4%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接相关的技术参考。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文讨论的是LLM服务经济学问题，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无技术关联。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

</details>


### [152] [RENEW: Towards Learning World Models and Repairing Model Exploitation from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14180)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Mykel Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于人类偏好的世界模型动力学修复方法（DLHF/RENEW），用于离线强化学习中模型利用问题的缓解。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全属于离线强化学习（offline RL）领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离线强化学习中的世界模型动力学学习与修复。应用场景：Jumanji和经典控制环境。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及世界模型（world model）和基于Bradley-Terry偏好损失的动力学模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Jumanji环境和经典控制环境。评价指标：样本效率（sample efficiency）、灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）、模型利用程度（exploitation）。

Experiments: 在Jumanji和经典控制环境上评估，naive DLHF需要大量偏好预算，RENEW提升了样本效率、限制了灾难性遗忘、减少了预训练世界模型中的利用问题。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于离线强化学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: World models are widely used in offline reinforcement learning (RL) to improve sample efficiency and generate experience beyond a fixed dataset. However, they are vulnerable to model exploitation where data coverage is thin. Prior work addresses this either by collecting more expert demonstrations, which is often expensive, unsafe, or unavailable, or by conservative algorithms that avoid uncertain regions, which limits generalization. We propose instead to repair exploitation directly using human preferences over imagined rollouts, leveraging the strong intuitive physics that allows humans to easily spot egregious dynamics hallucinations. We formalize this as Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF), a Bradley-Terry preference loss over trajectory log-likelihoods under a learned dynamics model. Unfortunately, naive DLHF is sample inefficient, so we introduce RENEW, which uses epistemic uncertainty to focus finetuning where the model is most exploitable. We evaluate on several Jumanji and classic control environments and find that while naive DLHF requires an outsize preference budget, RENEW makes the framework practical by improving sample efficiency, limiting catastrophic forgetting, and reducing exploitation in pretrained world models. Taken together, our results provide initial evidence that preferences can supervise world model dynamics directly, offering a new approach to addressing exploitation in offline model-based RL.

</details>


### [153] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将高阶Runge-Kutta积分器应用于Adam优化器（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对），但在严格等计算量对比下，RK变体在训练损失上不如普通Adam，其"自适应性"是虚假的，实际退化为固定步长Adam且计算成本增加3-4倍；修复后虽能大幅降低训练损失，但无法提升测试精度，最终结论是高阶自适应积分带来的收益无法超越精心调优的一阶基线方法。

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>

Abstract: Feedback-driven loops support iterative improvement in large language models, reinforcement learning, and autonomous discovery, yet their gains often diminish under repeated internal feedback. We study why closed-loop knowledge systems saturate and what external information can move them beyond their current attractors. We introduce a three-level operational framework in which knowledge states $x_t$ evolve through transition kernels $K_θ$ indexed by a structural parameter $θ$. The governing structure is defined as the observational equivalence class of $θ$ induced by these kernels, while attractors and basins are properties of the fixed-$θ$ dynamics. A structural intervention changes $θ$ and produces a detectable kernel discrepancy on pre-specified probe states, making structural change falsifiable. Using a Lyapunov drift condition, we show that stable internal dynamics approach bounded stability regions with exponentially attenuated transients and a noise-controlled residual floor. We characterize escape through a metric condition on intervention-induced attractor displacement and a baseline-relative KL lower bound for increasing escape probability. This analysis also explains why conditional mutual information alone cannot certify escape: it measures variation among intervention-conditioned updates rather than departure from the no-intervention law. Case studies in LLM code repair, sparse-reward reinforcement learning, and Bayesian optimization use matched continuation controls to illustrate how feedback strength and alignment affect quality-improving escape. Our contribution is an operational connection among stability tools, measurable intervention effects, and cross-domain diagnostics.

</details>


### [155] [Probabilistic Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Estimating Heterogeneous Elastic Properties from Low-Resolution and Noisy Displacement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14563)
*Tatthapong Srikitrungruang,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络（PIE-PINN），用于从低分辨率、含噪声的位移测量数据中鲁棒估计杨氏模量和泊松比。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: PIE-PINN框架：B样条引导的位移网络（B-spline-guided displacement network）+ 神经网络校正 + 层次半柯西模型（hierarchical half-Cauchy model）。基于Laplace分布的联合概率模型建模位移观测、应变差异和平衡残差。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向力学仿真/反问题求解，不涉及星上或边缘部署。
