Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [202] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非对称峰值感知损失函数(APAL)，用于时间序列预测中罕见需求尖峰的准确预测，主要面向人群需求预测等运营场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于时间序列预测（time-series forecasting）和人群需求预测（crowd demand forecasting），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列预测（time-series forecasting）；应用场景：人群需求预测（crowd demand forecasting）、海滩访客计数；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文声称APAL是model-agnostic（模型无关的）损失函数，在五个SOTA backbone上进行了评估，但未列出具体backbone名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset（生产级子集）、beach visitor count dataset，以及额外的时间序列基准数据集；评价指标：MAE、MSE、通道级尾部误差（Top-10%、Top-1%）、峰值精度/召回率/F1（含时间容差）、峰值时序误差（peak timing error）。

Experiments: 在五个SOTA backbone上评估APAL，在峰值关键数据集上提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，但会与聚合误差存在可控的权衡。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何技术参考。摘要未说明损失函数在视觉任务（如目标检测）上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然APAL中"对欠预测施加更高惩罚"的非对称损失思想在理论上可迁移到遥感小目标检测中（例如对漏检小目标施加更高损失权重），但论文本身未涉及任何视觉或遥感场景验证，迁移可行性未知，且不涉及轻量化，因此对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [203] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究的是随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标的PAC学习问题，提出去中心化、私有信息下的学习算法，与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于强化学习/博弈论理论方向，研究的是turn-based stochastic games中的reachability objectives的PAC学习问题。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究方向与本课题（遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化）完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [204] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究对象是大语言模型的意识形态泛化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署中的任何一项。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：经济学Q&A、职场HR政策、金融查询、食品安全等文本数据集。评价指标：意识形态偏移的广度（breadth）和放大效应（amplification），GSM8K准确率。不涉及任何遥感数据集。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP安全领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署中的任何核心技术。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。纯理论推导，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文为纯数学理论分析，未涉及任何实际应用场景、数据集验证或计算效率考量，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：分类器线性组合的权重优化思想或许可类比于模型集成（ensemble）中的加权融合策略，但该论文是纯理论分析，且面向二分类器组合，与遥感目标检测的轻量化部署需求相距甚远。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [206] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本方法，利用已有监督微调数据（含人工编写的解决方案）进一步提升推理语言模型（RLM）在可验证和难验证领域的性能，通过指令微调+模型合并实现。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力提升，涉及指令微调和模型合并技术，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：推理语言模型（RLM）性能提升；应用场景：数学、代码、文本摘要等NLP领域；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于已有的推理语言模型（RLM），先进行指令微调（instruction tuning），再与原始推理模型进行模型合并（model merging）。具体架构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。提及在coding和text summarization领域进行评估。

Experiments: 在可验证领域（coding）和难验证领域（text summarization）上验证了方法有效性，同时保持RLM在其他领域的能力。方法成本极低（< USD $3）。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化等本研究方向内容；2. 方法仅针对NLP领域的推理语言模型，无法直接迁移到视觉目标检测任务；3. 摘要未说明在遥感或视觉任务上的任何实验结果。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是"模型合并"（model merging）思想——如果未来在遥感目标检测轻量化中考虑将轻量模型与原始模型进行某种融合以恢复精度，但该论文未提供任何视觉领域的证据，且与轻量化（压缩/加速）目标相悖。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [207] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机对数缩放(RLS)方法，一种即插即用的后处理防御策略，用于抵御黑盒分数型对抗样本攻击，同时保持模型精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于对抗样本攻击与防御（黑盒场景下的score-based攻击），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"轻量化"一词在摘要中未出现，但该工作属于对抗鲁棒性领域，与在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非研究方向同时涉及对抗鲁棒性，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：对抗样本攻击与防御（黑盒score-based攻击）；应用场景：通用机器学习模型的安全部署；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。RLS作为后处理模块可加在任意已有ML模型之上，属于plug-and-play设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。RLS是一种随机化防御策略（随机缩放logits），与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。RLS作为后处理模块计算开销可能较低，但论文未给出定量分析。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。提及的指标包括：攻击成功率（attack success rate）、模型精度（accuracy）、置信度分数失真（confidence score distortion）。

Experiments: 实验表明RLS能显著降低SOTA黑盒score-based攻击的成功率，同时保持模型精度，并相比其他随机化防御方法减少置信度分数失真。此外，对另一种非随机化防御AAA提出了自适应攻击，揭示了其脆弱性。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 未讨论轻量化或边缘部署问题；3. 未给出计算开销（FLOPs/延迟）定量分析；4. 仅针对黑盒score-based攻击，对其他类型攻击（如transfer-based、decision-based）的防御效果未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：如果在轨遥感目标检测模型需要考虑对抗鲁棒性（例如星上模型受到干扰），RLS作为一种轻量级后处理防御（随机缩放logits）可以低成本集成到现有检测模型中，但论文本身未涉及遥感或检测场景。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [208] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaBase，将动力系统零样本重建的SOTA模型DynaMix压缩为仅含2个参数的极简可解释模型，在混沌和周期系统上仍保持高竞争力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是动力系统（dynamical systems）的零样本重建与可解释性压缩，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然文中提到了"reduce"和"parameter load"，但这是针对动力系统重建模型的参数简化，与遥感目标检测模型的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：动力系统（dynamical systems）的零样本重建与预测；场景：混沌系统、周期系统；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DynaBase：仅含2个参数的线性模型，通过当前隐状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继的线性混合进行预测。是DynaMix（Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025）的迭代简化版本。

Lightweight method: 本文的"轻量化"是指通过理论分析和迭代简化，将复杂的基础模型（foundation model）缩减为2参数的可解释模型。这与遥感领域的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）完全不同，属于数学层面的模型约简，而非工程部署层面的压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。虽然参数极少（2个参数），但应用场景是动力系统重建，非遥感在轨场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：混沌系统和周期系统的动力系统数据；评价指标：零样本重建质量、预测MSE（均方误差）。

Experiments: DynaBase在混沌和周期系统的零样本重建上具有高度竞争力，参数负载比其它基础模型低多个数量级。支持闭式一步解析解优化。不同训练策略可得到短期预测最优或动力系统重建最优的模型。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 方法针对动力系统的时间序列预测，非图像空间检测任务；3. 没有涉及任何遥感领域的数据集或评价指标；4. 没有讨论在轨部署、实时推理、硬件适配等问题。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的启发是：对于复杂模型，可以通过理论分析找到其核心机制并大幅简化，但这一思路在遥感目标检测领域已有大量工作（如轻量骨干网络设计），且本文方法针对的是时间序列动力系统，与遥感图像目标检测的空间特征提取完全不同。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [209] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时序观测数据中带有结果干扰的因果推断，使用基于Ising模型的马尔可夫过程和最大伪似然估计（MPLE）进行参数学习与因果效应估计，并在COVID-19疫苗率与死亡率数据上进行了验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于因果推断（causal inference）和统计学习，不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、目标检测（object detection）、轻量化（lightweight）或星上部署（onboard deployment）等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果推断（causal inference）；应用场景：公共卫生（COVID-19疫苗效果分析）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Ising模型的马尔可夫过程，结合低秩因子结构处理潜在混杂因素，使用最大伪似然估计（MPLE）进行参数学习。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 合成实验数据 + 美国各县COVID-19疫苗率与死亡率的真实数据。评价指标为因果效应估计的准确性（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 通过合成实验验证方法有效性，并在美国各县COVID-19疫苗率对死亡率的因果效应案例研究中展示了实际应用效果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于因果推断与统计学习领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [210] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongStraw，一个面向百万token级RL后训练（GRPO）的架构感知执行栈，针对长上下文场景优化GPU内存效率，但完全不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的长上下文强化学习后训练（RL post-training）的内存优化，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或任何视觉任务。虽然涉及"轻量化"概念（减少训练图大小、优化显存），但这是针对LLM训练而非遥感目标检测模型的推理/部署轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长上下文LLM的RL后训练（GRPO）；应用场景：AI agent长轨迹推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感或视觉相关内容。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen3.6-27B（混合循环+全注意力）和GLM-5.2（压缩注意力MoE）实现。核心设计：共享prompt无autograd评估、仅保留后续token需要的模型状态、逐次回放短响应分支以减少实时训练图大小。

Lightweight method: 不涉及遥感模型轻量化。本文的"轻量化"体现在训练图的内存优化：通过分离共享prompt的梯度计算、状态保留策略、响应分支回放，以额外时间换取显存节省。这与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关内容。实验在8块/32块H20 GPU上进行，关注训练显存而非推理部署指标。参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等均未提及。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：峰值显存分配（peak allocated memory）、可处理的最大token位置数（2.1M/4.46M positions）。

Experiments: 在8块H20 GPU上：Qwen和GLM的grouped scoring和response backward达到2.1M positions（group size 2和8）；增大group size仅增加0.21 GB峰值显存；压力测试达4.46M positions。在32块H20 GPU上：验证GLM-5.2全部78层的2.1M-token prompt端到端执行路径。作者明确说明这些实验验证的是执行容量而非完整训练正确性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 实验仅验证执行容量，未验证完整训练正确性；3. 分布式前向和梯度组合路径仍不完整；4. 针对LLM训练场景，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测模型轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其"以时间换空间"的思路（replay short response branches），在星上资源受限场景中，如果推理延迟不是硬实时约束，可以考虑类似的分阶段计算策略。但整体而言，本文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向几乎无直接关联。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [211] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多轴max@K强化学习目标，用于提升扩散T2I模型在预设语义模式上的图像生成覆盖率与公平性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于Text-to-Image生成模型的多样性覆盖与公平性问题，属于生成式视觉/多模态领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：文本到图像生成（Text-to-Image generation）的多样性覆盖与公平性优化。场景：人物图像生成（person-centric prompts）的视觉模式覆盖与人口统计偏差缓解。传感器/数据类型：自然图像（非遥感）。目标类别：预设语义类别（如肤色、外观属性）。

Model architecture: 基于扩散模型（diffusion-based T2I models），具体实验使用SD3.5-M。核心模块：多轴max@K组级强化学习目标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向通用图像生成，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成混合数据（synthetic mixture）及SD3.5-M生成数据。评价指标：Fairness Score（公平性分数），图像质量（image quality），文本对齐度（text alignment）。自动评估器（automatic evaluators）在held-out prompts上评测。

Experiments: 在合成数据上验证信用分配机制（credit-assignment mechanism）；在SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素级颜色奖励（deterministic pixel-based color rewards）评估；在感知外观公平性（perceived-appearance fairness）上，多轴max@K相比基础模型在Fairness Score上提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 未讨论计算效率、模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。3. 仅针对人物生成场景的公平性，泛化性有限。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）组级奖励分配（group-based credit assignment）思想可类比于多目标检测任务中不同类别样本的协同优化；2）多轴max@K中"不同样本贡献不同类别"的思路，或可迁移到遥感多类别目标检测的样本权重设计。但整体方向差异大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [212] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态语义感知对比学习框架(MseaCL)，通过引入语义相似度缓解医学影像中的假负样本问题，提升下游脑肿瘤分子分类性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学影像（3D脑MRI）与放射学报告的多模态对比学习，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态对比学习预训练 + 下游脑肿瘤分子分类；场景：医疗影像（儿科3D脑MRI + 放射学报告）；传感器：MRI；目标类别：脑肿瘤分子亚型。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: MseaCL框架，基于对比学习，引入语义相似度作为引导信号来缓解假负样本问题。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：儿科3D脑MRI扫描与放射学报告（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：AUC（ROC曲线下面积），下游脑肿瘤分子分类任务上提升至少22.6%。

Experiments: 将MseaCL作为预训练阶段，在下游脑肿瘤分子分类任务上AUC提升至少22.6%。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向医疗场景，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化均无关；2. 未提供模型复杂度、推理速度等部署指标；3. 仅在单一儿科脑MRI数据集上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果硬要提取思路：对比学习中的假负样本缓解策略（语义感知负样本筛选）在遥感多模态（如光学-SAR配准、遥感图像-文本描述）中可能有潜在借鉴意义，但本文未涉及遥感场景，且不涉及任何轻量化设计，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接帮助很小。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [213] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [214] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于SINDy（稀疏非线性动力学识别）方法的教程，介绍其在工程问题中从数据中恢复可解释控制方程的应用，并给出无人机系统辨识和热交换器两个案例。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是系统辨识/动力学建模（SINDy方法），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心方向。虽然案例中出现了无人机（UAV），但这是作为动力学系统辨识的对象，而非遥感图像中的目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 核心方法是SINDy（Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics），通过稀疏回归从候选非线性项库中恢复控制方程。扩展包括弱形式（weak-form）、集成（ensembling）、约束（constrained）和参数化（parametrizable）变体。不涉及神经网络或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文讨论的是稀疏回归方法本身，与模型剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等轻量化技术无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用两个案例研究——(1) 无人机的系统辨识，(2) 混沌热虹吸热交换器。未使用遥感标准数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 通过两个工程案例（无人机系统辨识、热交换器）展示SINDy方法的应用效果。未与目标检测方法进行对比，未进行消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。SINDy方法面向的是物理系统的控制方程发现，而非视觉感知任务。无法为遥感目标检测模型提供直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：SINDy的稀疏性思想（sparse regression）与模型剪枝（pruning）在"减少冗余"的理念上有一定类比，但方法论完全不同，且不涉及神经网络。不建议作为主要参考论文。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>

*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文是纯范畴论/数学基础研究，讨论的是机器学习问题的形式化框架（sketch理论、切空间、组合性），不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署的具体技术。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [217] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向大语言模型推理能力迁移的在线策略增量蒸馏方法（OPD²），通过定义教师模型与基座模型之间的"delta信号"作为蒸馏奖励，提升推理能力蒸馏效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏，属于自然语言处理和强化学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型推理能力蒸馏（数学、科学、代码推理）；应用场景：LLM后训练阶段；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 教师模型为经过推理指令微调的LLM，学生模型通过在线策略蒸馏学习；核心创新是delta信号（教师模型与基座模型在推理微调前后的输出差异）。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），但面向的是LLM推理能力迁移，而非遥感目标检测模型的轻量化。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在数学、科学、代码推理基准上进行，具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: OPD²在多个推理基准上持续优于传统在线策略蒸馏方法，且仅需较短的后训练时间即可达到强性能。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 仅针对LLM推理能力，与视觉模型轻量化无关；3. 未讨论部署效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但"delta信号"的思想——即利用教师模型微调前后的差异作为蒸馏目标——在概念上可迁移思考：在遥感目标检测模型蒸馏中，是否可以定义"教师模型在特定遥感数据集上微调前后的检测能力差异"作为更直接的蒸馏信号？不过这一思路需要大量适配工作。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [218] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，用神经网络替代未知的ODE分量，通过RTS平滑器和反向传播交替估计状态与参数，从部分观测中学习缺失的动力学方程。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于物理/生物/神经科学中的微分方程建模与状态估计，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为ODE缺失分量学习与状态估计，应用场景为物理/生物/神经科学中的动力系统建模，传感器类型和目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: 混合神经-物理框架：已知ODE分量保持显式，缺失分量由神经网络表示。两阶段交替迭代：第一阶段用RTS smoother推断隐状态，第二阶段用平滑轨迹通过反向传播训练神经网络参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 在基准动力系统（线性、非线性、刚性动力学）上评估，部分状态观测设置。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在多种动力学系统上验证，方法能从不完整测量中学习缺失ODE分量，保留可解释的机械结构，改善隐状态重建和长时预测。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的检测或轻量化技术。方法针对连续动力系统建模，不涉及离散图像特征提取或目标定位分类。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是"混合模型"思路（物理先验+数据驱动），但该思路在遥感领域已有广泛研究，且本文不涉及图像/检测/轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [219] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KP-tree的可变草图结构，用于解决推荐系统中用户嵌入陈旧性问题，实现实时增量更新和快速个性化推荐。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是推荐系统（recommendation system）中的用户嵌入更新问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。属于泛机器学习/推荐系统方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：推荐系统（recommendation）；应用场景：用户-物品评分预测；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: KP-tree（稀疏线段树+求和聚合），低秩投影（low-rank projection），可变草图（mutable sketches）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。虽然提到了低秩投影和稀疏数据结构，但这是推荐系统领域的效率优化，非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署。虽然提到了8x faster per-batch updates和<1 ms个性化推荐，但这是推荐系统场景的延迟指标，与遥感在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KuaiRec；评价指标：RMSE（0.810 vs 0.822）、数据读取量（1.8% vs 100%）、更新速度（8x faster）、物品覆盖率（40-130% better）。

Experiments: 在KuaiRec上与ALS对比，可变草图以1.8%数据读取达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822 RMSE at 100%数据），每批次更新快8倍。新用户首次评分后<1 ms获得个性化推荐。稀疏数据下KP-tree的norm-proportional采样比均匀采样物品覆盖率高40-130%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要本身未讨论遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。KP-tree的稀疏索引和增量更新思想（on-the-fly recomputation）在理论上可类比于遥感在轨场景中模型参数的增量更新或特征图的稀疏计算，但需要大幅改造才能适配目标检测任务。整体借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>

*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据、Twitter每日情绪分类数据；评价指标：F1-score（约0.84）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和规模。

Experiments: 使用交叉验证测试了多种机器学习模型，XGBoost在情绪分类任务上取得最佳平均F1-score约0.84；使用SHAP进行特征贡献度分析。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>

### [221] [Tight Wavelet Frames on Graphs via Isometric Group Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14168)
*Rigobert Fokam Souop,Laurent Bitjoka*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Cayley图嵌入的谱图小波构造方法，通过将图嵌入到有限阿贝尔群的Cayley图中，利用宿主上的经典傅里叶分析构造紧框架小波，实现图信号的精确重构与多分辨率分解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯图信号处理/谱图小波理论工作，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署中的任何一个核心要素。既无遥感图像/卫星数据，也无目标检测任务，更无模型轻量化或边缘部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。纯图信号处理理论工作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为图信号的多分辨率分析与重构，应用场景为图信号处理（graph signal processing），不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 提出两种小波构造方法：(1) Dilation wavelets——利用群自同构实现经典平移-膨胀模板；(2) Spectral band-pass wavelets——在频域幅度上使用归一化滤波器组，形成Parseval紧框架。整体变换复杂度为O(JN log N)，通过宿主快速傅里叶变换实现。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用benchmark hosts进行实验，未提及遥感领域常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）或检测指标（mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 在benchmark hosts上重建达到机器精度，band-pass原子在距中心图距离2范围内集中89-99%的能量。未提供与遥感目标检测相关的实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 需要图到Cayley群的等距嵌入，对非规则图结构存在嵌入约束；(3) 未讨论该方法如何应用于图像/视觉任务；(4) 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：(1) 图小波变换的O(JN log N)复杂度思路可用于图神经网络加速；(2) 紧框架的精确重构性质可启发遥感特征提取中的信息无损下采样设计；(3) 离散调和延拓作为信号补全的规范方法，可借鉴于遥感图像中的缺失区域处理。但这些均为间接联想，与在轨目标检测轻量化的核心课题距离较远。

Abstract: Spectral graph wavelets apply a kernel to the graph Laplacian spectrum. On an irregular graph their analyzing functions inherit a non-canonical eigenbasis, they do not form a tight frame, and reconstruction requires inverting a frame operator. We take a different route, built on an exact substrate. Given an isometric embedding of a connected graph into a Cayley graph of a finite abelian group, a host on which classical Fourier analysis applies exactly, we construct wavelets on the host and restrict them to the graph. Two constructions arise and we keep them separate. Dilation wavelets use a group automorphism as a dilation, reproducing the classical translate-dilate template but existing only on hosts with composite cyclic factors. Spectral band-pass wavelets use a normalized filter bank in the dual frequency magnitude; they exist on every host, form a Parseval (tight) frame, reconstruct any graph signal exactly via restriction, are translation-covariant, and localize jointly in vertex and frequency. We prove the tight-frame identity and exact reconstruction, give a multiresolution decomposition, and show the full transform costs O(JN log N) via the host fast Fourier transform. For a proper embedding we show the canonical way to complete a signal onto the host remainder is the discrete harmonic extension, which uniquely minimizes the host Dirichlet energy and places the zero-padding and symmetric-extension heuristics as approximations of it. On benchmark hosts reconstruction reaches machine precision and band-pass atoms concentrate 89-99 percent of their energy within graph-distance two of their center.

</details>


### [222] [Denoising-Autoencoder-Assisted Physical Layer Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14505)
*Zhengyu Xu,Shun Kojima,Shinya Sugiura*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于无线通信安全领域的密钥生成研究。

Model architecture: 去噪自编码器(DAE)，用于捕获输入分布的内在结构、重构原始数据、去除加性噪声。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关信息。

Experiments: 所提方案在KDR、SKC和密钥随机性上优于传统方案，但具体数值和对比方法摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的模型压缩、检测架构或遥感数据处理内容。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose denoising autoencoder (DAE)-assisted secret key generation (SKG), where channel noise reciprocity imperfections induced due to wireless channel measurements are suppressed, hence significantly enhancing the reliability and efficiency. More specifically, the DAE is capable of capturing the intrinsic structure of input distributions, reconstructing the original data structure, and removing additive noise while preserving the essential structure of signals. In our analysis, it is demonstrated that the proposed SKG scheme exhibits higher performance than the conventional schemes in terms of key disagreement rate (KDR), secret key capacity (SKC), and randomness of the generated keys.

</details>


### [223] [SLIPT-Enabled Ground-to-UAV FSO Systems with Optical Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14523)

Abstract: This paper proposes an optical reconfigurable intelligent surface (ORIS)-assisted ground-to-unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) free-space optical (FSO) communication system empowered by simultaneous lightwave information and power transfer (SLIPT). To overcome the line-of-sight (LoS) limitation of FSO-based SLIPT systems, we introduce an ORIS that reflects the laser beam towards a non-LoS UAV receiver. We model and analyze the combined channel characteristics, incorporating atmospheric loss, turbulence-induced fading, pointing error, and angle-of-arrival fluctuations due to UAV hovering. We derive closed-form expressions for harvested energy, outage probability, and symbol error rate (SER). Numerical results show that integrating ORIS improves EH efficiency while maintaining manageable outage and SER performance.

</details>


### [224] [Adaptive Score-Based VAMP: Self-Tuning Hyperparameters via Tilted EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14597)
*Siqi Na,Tadashi Wadayama*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应SC-VAMP方法，通过局部倾斜EM步骤更新参数化因子，实现高维逆问题中的贝叶斯最优推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于压缩感知和贝叶斯推理中的近似消息传递方法，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高维逆问题的贝叶斯推理；应用场景：线性/单比特伯努利-高斯压缩感知；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: SC-VAMP（score-based vector approximate message passing），包含先验模块和似然/LMMSE模块，采用单输入单输出模块接口。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数值实验：线性压缩感知和单比特伯努利-高斯压缩感知；评价指标：近最优（near-oracle）恢复性能。

Experiments: 在强失配初始化条件下，所提自适应更新方法能够恢复近最优性能。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于信号处理/压缩感知理论方向，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Approximate-message-passing methods offer fast Bayesian inference for high-dimensional inverse problems, but their performance and state-evolution predictions rely on correctly specified module parameters. This paper develops an adaptive version of score-based vector approximate message passing (SC-VAMP). Each parameterized factor is updated by a local tilted expectation-maximization (EM) step that reuses the tilted moments already computed by the single-input single-output module interface. Under standard large-system state-evolution assumptions and identifiability conditions, the matched parameters form a Bayes-optimal population fixed point of the adaptive recursion. The argument is written separately for prior modules and likelihood/LMMSE modules, the latter using the Gaussian cavity induced by the VAMP transformed-error model. Numerical results for linear and one-bit Bernoulli-Gaussian compressed sensing show that the proposed updates recover near-oracle performance from strongly mismatched initializations.

</details>


### [225] [Efficient Quantum Algorithm for Phase Optimization of 1-Bit RIS-Assisted MIMO Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14700)
*Soumyadip Paul,Neel Kanth Kundu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于量子近似优化算法（QAOA-LR）的1-bit RIS辅助MIMO通信系统相位优化方法，用固定线性斜坡替代经典优化器，在IBM量子处理器上验证了近ML性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是无线通信领域的RIS（可重构智能表面）相位优化问题，属于量子计算+通信的交叉方向，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：RIS辅助MIMO通信系统的被动波束赋形设计（组合优化问题）。应用场景：无线通信。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: QAOA-LR（Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with Linear Ramp），p层变分量子电路结构，使用固定线性斜坡调度设置变分参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法。文中提到的"降低计算复杂度"是指相对于经典优化方法的量子加速，而非深度学习模型的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署。实验在IBM量子处理器上进行，关注的是量子处理单元执行时间的多项式缩放，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 蒙特卡洛仿真（Rayleigh衰落MIMO信道），IBM量子处理器硬件实验。评价指标：与最优最大似然（ML）解的逼近程度、容量性能、计算复杂度、量子处理单元执行时间。

Experiments: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明QAOA-LR紧密跟踪最优ML解；IBM量子处理器硬件实验验证了近ML容量性能，且量子处理单元执行时间随RIS单元数呈多项式缩放。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于量子计算+无线通信领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We propose a Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a deterministic linear ramp schedule (QAOA-LR) for phase optimization of a 1-bit RIS-assisted MIMO communication system. Each RIS element is restricted to a binary phase shift of 0 or π, turning the passive beamforming design problem with N elements into a combinatorial optimization problem over 2^N configurations. Instead of running a classical optimizer, QAOA-LR uses a fixed linear ramp to set the variational parameters across p layers and finds the best scale via a simple one-dimensional grid search over a single parameter. Monte Carlo simulations over Rayleigh-fading MIMO channels confirm that QAOA-LR closely tracks the optimum maximum-likelihood (ML) solution. Furthermore, the proposed algorithm reduces the computational complexity compared with classical optimization, and real hardware experiments on the IBM Quantum processor confirm near-ML capacity performance with polynomial scaling of quantum processing unit execution time as the number of RIS elements increases.

</details>


### [226] [Effect of Antenna Deployment on Achievable Rate in Cooperative Magnetic Induction Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14701)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Rui Wang,Xinyu Qu*

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于矿井/地下河等穿地场景的磁感应（MI）通信中继方案，通过放大转发（AF）中继提升传输速率，并推导了中继天线部署（AD）对数据速率增益和信道带宽的闭式表达式。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是通信中继方案（cooperative MI with AF relay），而非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩方法。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴的技术信息。

Abstract: Magnetic Induction (MI) communication can be applied in some through-the-earth scenarios such as mines and underground rivers. To increase the transmission rate of MI communications, we propose a cooperative MI (CMI) scheme with an amplify-and-forward (AF) relay. Different from existing studies, we mainly focus on the relay with arbitrary antenna position and orientation (antenna deployment, AD). We derive the closed-form expression of CMI achievable data rate gain (CMG) for the relay and the closed-form expression of CMI channel bandwidth. Simulations reveal that a relay with appropriate AD could yield a significant increase in the achievable rate for MI systems.

</details>


### [227] [Quantifying the complexity of trajectory ensembles with clustering-weighted multivariate multiscale sample entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14738)

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。虽然摘要中提到了"satellite constellations"（卫星星座）作为应用场景之一，但论文核心是时间序列/轨迹集合的熵度量方法，属于信息论与复杂系统分析范畴，不涉及目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列/轨迹集合的群体复杂度度量；应用场景：物理科学、生命科学中的轨迹数据分析（包括卫星星座轨迹、混沌流、临床队列等）；传感器类型：不特定；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是统计度量方法（CWMMSE），而非深度学习模型架构。

Experiments: 在11个跨领域数据集上验证，包括：平静海洋区域与高能海洋区域的复杂度排序、大地震作为系统复杂度崩溃的识别、心脏队列中疾病降低群体多样性的发现等。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的技术参考。摘要本身未说明该方法在遥感图像目标检测任务上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的借鉴点是：论文提到"satellite constellations"作为轨迹数据来源之一，如果研究方向涉及卫星轨迹数据分析（而非遥感图像目标检测），可考虑将CWMMSE用于卫星集群行为模式的复杂度分析。但对于"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"这一具体课题，本论文无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Across the physical and life sciences, data increasingly appear as ensembles of trajectories, from chaotic flows and satellite constellations to clinical cohorts. Established sample-entropy measures characterize individual time series, while averaging across an ensemble discards population structure and cannot distinguish redundancy from diversity. We introduce clustering-weighted multivariate multiscale sample entropy (CWMMSE), which groups trajectories into behavioral patterns and weights each by its dynamical complexity. CWMMSE is a weighted entropy of the population's pattern distribution. Its empirical plug-in estimator is strongly consistent for a fixed finite partition, and it separates two components that can diverge in real data: individual complexity and population diversity. Both are essential. Averaging ignores diversity, whereas spread alone can mistake a varied but predictable population for a complex one. Across eleven physical, environmental, engineering, and biomedical systems, CWMMSE ranks a calm ocean region above an energetic but individually more complex one, identifies a major earthquake as a collapse in system complexity, and reverses the conclusion from averaging in cardiac cohorts, where disease reduces population diversity. Supported by an open, reproducible implementation, these results show that population complexity should be measured rather than averaged.

</details>


### [228] [Elliptic Range-Doppler Mapping for OFDM-ISAC under IQ Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14775)
*Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Dimitrios Bozanis,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对OFDM-ISAC系统中接收机IQ不平衡导致的镜像干扰问题，提出椭圆组正交匹配追踪检测器，直接在受损观测上进行稀疏恢复，无需先补偿IQI。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是OFDM-ISAC（通感一体化）系统中的IQI补偿与目标检测问题，属于通信与雷达感知交叉领域，不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR图像、目标检测模型或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：OFDM-ISAC系统中的目标检测（稀疏恢复）；应用场景：通信感知一体化；传感器类型：OFDM雷达/通信信号；目标类别：物理目标（延迟-多普勒域）。不涉及遥感图像、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱等。

Model architecture: 椭圆组正交匹配追踪（Elliptic Group OMP）检测器，基于IQI受损观测的椭圆原子组表示，通过加权二维FFT和局部组投影实现高效相关计算。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提到通过加权二维FFT实现高效计算，但这是算法层面的计算效率优化，非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及使用加权二维FFT实现高效计算，但未给出具体效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称，使用数值仿真（Numerical results）；评价指标：精确支持恢复（exact support recovery）、弱目标检测性能（weak-target detection）。

Experiments: 与对应基准方法对比，在中度和强IQI条件下，所提方法在精确支持恢复和弱目标检测方面有改进。未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对OFDM-ISAC场景，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联；2. 仅在数值仿真中验证，缺乏真实遥感数据实验；3. 未讨论模型复杂度、参数量、推理速度等部署关键指标；4. 未涉及任何轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可参考的是：在信号处理层面，利用结构化稀疏表示（椭圆原子组）来处理传感器非理想特性（如IQI）的思路，类比于遥感卫星中处理传感器噪声、辐射畸变等问题时可采用结构化稀疏方法。但与本课题的"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"核心方向关联极弱。

Abstract: Receiver in-phase/quadrature imbalance (IQI) couples each OFDM subcarrier with its mirror counterpart, creating ghost targets and degrading range-Doppler recovery in orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Instead of first compensating for IQI and then applying conventional processing, this letter exploits the structure of the IQI-impaired observation directly. We show that each physical target induces coupled direct and mirror components linked through the target coefficient and its conjugate, which motivates an elliptic atom group representation for each candidate delay-Doppler cell. Based on this model, we propose an elliptic group orthogonal matching pursuit detector that performs sparse recovery directly on the received OFDM grid. The required correlations are computed efficiently through two weighted two-dimensional fast Fourier transforms (FFTs) followed by local group projections. Numerical results show that the proposed method improves exact support recovery and weak-target detection compared to corresponding benchmarks, especially under moderate and strong receiver IQI.

</details>


### [229] [Conditional Generative Learning Enabled Wireless UAV Sensing and Tracking via Point Cloud Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14778)
*Xinhong Dai,Yuan Gao,Hao Jiang,Xiaojun Yuan,Xin Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于天线阵列回波信号的无人机感知与跟踪方法（AUGUST），利用条件通道编码和扩散生成模型实现无人机电磁点云成像与位姿估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是地面基站天线阵列对无人机的感知与跟踪（电磁点云成像），不涉及遥感卫星、卫星在轨处理、光学/SAR遥感图像目标检测，也不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究的是地面天线阵列对无人机的电磁点云成像与跟踪，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：UAV sensing and tracking（无人机感知与跟踪）；应用场景：地面基站天线阵列对空中无人机的探测；传感器类型：antenna array（天线阵列），非光学/SAR/高光谱遥感传感器；目标类别：UAV（无人机），非遥感图像中的舰船/飞机/车辆等；与遥感卫星场景无关。

Model architecture: AUGUST框架包含两部分：1）条件通道编码模块（conditional channel encoding module），含position embedding和SNR embedding；2）生成解码模块（generative decoding module），基于diffusion model，带加权训练目标。整体为生成式点云重建架构，非目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向地面基站处理，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真数据（simulation），非公开遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、LEVIR等）；评价指标：点云重建 fidelity（保真度）、UAV attitude and shape信息捕获精度、定位性能（positioning performance）。

Experiments: 与benchmark方案对比，AUGUST方法重建的点云保真度更高，能更准确捕获无人机姿态和形状信息；相比传统基于模型的baseline，定位性能有显著提升。摘要未提供具体数值。

Limitations: 1）仅基于仿真验证，缺乏真实场景实验；2）diffusion model推理速度慢，不适用于实时在轨场景；3）与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关；4）未讨论模型复杂度与部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接借鉴价值。唯一可能的间接启发是：其条件编码模块中的position embedding和SNR embedding设计思路，在遥感图像多尺度特征融合或信噪比自适应处理中可能有参考意义，但整体相关性极低。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) sensing and tracking problem, where a base station equipped with an antenna array continuously illuminates a flying UAV and exploits the reflected echoes for slot-wise point cloud imaging within its potential flight region. To accomplish this task, the imaging region for each slot is determined based on the prior of the historical UAV positions. Then, the UAV is represented by an electromagnetic point cloud in this region that contains its spatial information and electromagnetic properties (EPs), enabling the unified extraction of UAV position, attitude, and shape from the reconstructed point cloud. The EP point cloud imaging for the UAV based on echo signals is a complex inverse problem. To this end, we propose an Array-based Unified Generative UAV Sensing and Tracking (AUGUST) approach, which integrates a conditional channel encoding module and a generative decoding module. The encoding module incorporates position and signal-to-noise ratio embeddings to stabilize the UAV intrinsic feature extraction under fast UAV position and channel variations, and maps the encoded features to a latent space regularized by a learnable flow-based prior. The decoding module employs a diffusion model with a weighted training objective to reconstruct the UAV point cloud guided by the extracted features. The simulation results demonstrate that the reconstructed point clouds via the proposed AUGUST approach present higher fidelity compared to the benchmark schemes, thereby enabling a more accurate capture of the UAV attitude and shape information. The AUGUST approach also presents a substantial gain over the conventional model-based baseline in positioning performance.

</details>


### [230] [Jacobi Elliptic Chirps for Sub-Nyquist Multi-Target Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14783)
*Dimitrios Bozanis,Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种正弦-余弦雅可比椭圆频率调制波形(SC-EFM)，用于解决亚奈奎斯特采样脉冲压缩雷达中鬼影目标检测与目标可分离性之间的权衡问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于雷达波形设计（sub-Nyquist采样、脉冲压缩雷达），属于雷达信号处理领域，不涉及遥感卫星光学/SAR图像、目标检测模型、轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：亚奈奎斯特采样下的多目标测距与鬼影抑制；应用场景：宽带脉冲压缩雷达；传感器类型：雷达（非遥感卫星SAR/光学传感器）；目标类别：雷达目标（非图像目标）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文核心是波形设计（SC-EFM波形），不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：多目标检测概率、鬼影目标检测概率、目标可分离性。

Experiments: 数值结果表明：SC-EFM相比LFM显著抑制鬼影检测，同时保持与LFM相当的目标可分离性；相比HFM在分辨邻近目标方面表现更优。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未讨论实际硬件实现复杂度；3. 仅针对雷达亚奈奎斯特采样场景，不涉及图像域目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：该论文通过波形设计解决"检测性能与分辨能力"的权衡，类似地，在遥感目标检测轻量化中也需要权衡"模型大小与检测精度"，但方法论完全不同。

Abstract: Sub-Nyquist sampling is an attractive way to reduce the hardware cost of wideband pulse-compression radar, but it introduces coherent alias-induced replicas in the matched-filter range profile, producing spurious peaks known as ghost targets. Existing frequency-modulated waveforms face a practical trade-off in this regime: linear frequency-modulated (LFM) pulses provide compact range responses but are highly susceptible to ghost-target detections, whereas hyperbolic frequency-modulated (HFM) pulses suppress ghosts at the cost of degraded target separability. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a sine-over-cosine Jacobi elliptic frequency-modulated waveform, referred to as SC-EFM, in which the elliptic modulus tunes the instantaneous-frequency (IF) curvature while preserving the pulse duration and bandwidth of conventional benchmarks. We characterize the sub-Nyquist folding structure of SC-EFM and derive closed-form expressions for the multi-target and ghost-target detection probabilities. Numerical results show that SC-EFM significantly suppresses ghost detections relative to LFM while matching its target separability, and substantially outperforms HFM in resolving close targets, providing a unified waveform solution for ghost-resilient sub-Nyquist multi-target ranging.

</details>


### [231] [LIVE-RIS: Real-Time In-Flight Actuation of UAV-Mounted RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14851)
*David Müller,Kevin Weinberger,Aydin Sezgin,Martin Mönnigmann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出首个搭载于无人机上的可重构智能表面(RIS)实时原型系统，通过无人机EKF和机载传感器预测RIS姿态，实现实时重配置以抵抗扰动，并通过实测验证性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文核心是6G无线通信中的RIS（可重构智能表面）技术，聚焦于无人机搭载RIS的通信链路性能验证，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心内容。虽然使用了无人机平台，但用途是通信中继而非遥感观测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：RIS辅助无线通信链路性能验证；应用场景：6G无线网络、无人机通信中继；传感器类型：无人机机载传感器（用于EKF姿态估计）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感、目标检测无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为RIS辅助链路的性能增益（如信号质量），不涉及mAP、FPS等目标检测指标。实验方式为实际硬件测量（experimental measurements）。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供目标检测或轻量化方面的局限性分析。论文自身的局限性可能包括：仅针对单一无人机平台、未考虑多RIS协同、实测场景有限等，但这些对遥感目标检测无参考价值。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) are emerging as a key technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks due to their ability to dynamically control the propagation environment. To ensure favorable Line-of-Sight (LoS) conditions in real-world applications, the RIS is mounted on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). While the potential of UAV-mounted RIS has been extensively studied in theoretical works, experimental validation with real-world data remains limited. Such validation is particularly important, as UAV motion and disturbances may degrade the performance of the RIS-enabled link. In this paper, we present the first fully functional, real-time capable UAV-mounted RIS prototype and validate its performance through experimental measurements under realistic disturbances and hardware constraints. We show that the RIS pose can be predicted based on the UAV's extended Kalman filter (EKF) and onboard sensors. By utilizing this estimation, we demonstrate that the RIS can be reconfigured in real time, effectively mitigating disturbance effects and preserving the performance gains of the RIS-enabled link. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate different deployment locations to provide insights into RIS performance in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [232] [Learning-Driven Channel Representation for Wireless Localization: From Channel Observations to Location Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14938)
*Hongyu Xie,Chenglong Li,Xinming Huang,Emmeric Tanghe,Wout Joseph,Shaojie Ni,Xiaojun Yuan*

Main category: eess.SP

Limitations: 摘要指出关键挑战包括：环境布局、时间动态、硬件差异、系统配置等因素导致性能一致性差；从实验研究到实际部署存在困难。但这些局限是针对无线定位场景的，与遥感目标检测无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：该论文强调"channel representation的质量和可用性对性能起决定性作用"，类比到遥感领域，可思考如何设计更好的特征表示来解耦遥感图像中的复杂背景与目标信息。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴点极少。

Abstract: Wireless observations capture radio signal responses formed through interactions with propagation environments and spatial geometry. In integrated sensing and communication, such observations have become an important basis for high-accuracy localization beyond conventional channel estimation. Learning-driven methods learn implicit relations between channel propagation and spatial position, enabling location inference under complex channel conditions. However, the useful information is tightly coupled with environmental layout, temporal dynamics, hardware differences, and system configurations. This coupling obscures the inference process and weakens performance consistency across scenarios. In this paper, we model the localization process as a unified ``wireless observation--channel representation--location inference'' framework, and review learning-driven high-accuracy localization techniques with channel representations as the organizing view. The survey covers typical channel observation forms and analyzes their physical meanings. We also review channel feature extraction and representation learning methods, and summarize methods according to the acquisition, organization, adaptation, and reuse of channel representations. Typical methods are compared in terms of accuracy, applicable conditions, data requirements, and generalization. We highlight that the quality and usability of channel representations are critical to exploiting propagation information, and thus play a decisive role in localization performance. Finally, we summarize the key challenges in moving from experimental studies to real deployment and present our perspectives on these issues.

</details>


### [233] [Achievable-Rate Analysis of MISO Systems with Transmit-Side Multiport Matching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14992)
*Wendong Cheng,Li Chen,Weidong Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究MISO系统中多端口匹配网络在Bode-Fano约束下的可达速率上界及电路实现方法，属于通信电路与信息论交叉领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦射频前端电路与通信理论，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文涉及多端口匹配网络(MMN)的电路结构，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是射频前端电路设计中的匹配网络优化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。
