Abstract: Characterizing communication performance under the physical constraints imposed by radio frequency front-end circuits is essential for bridging communication-theoretic analysis and practical circuit design. In this work, we investigate the achievable-rate upper bound of a multiple-input single-output (MISO) system with a transmitter-side interconnected multiport matching network (MMN) under multiport Bode--Fano constraints and develop a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method. First, based on a circuit-theoretic communication model and the concept of directional gain from multivariable control theory, we reveal how the directional characteristics of MMN power transmission interact with wireless propagation to affect communication performance. Then, building on this directional-gain interpretation, we reformulate the matrix-valued functional optimization problem that characterizes the achievable-rate upper bound and derive the optimal transmission-coefficient structure. Further, a greedy constraint-wise repair method is developed to obtain a feasible suboptimal solution. Finally, through a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method, we demonstrate that the derived theoretical insights provide effective guidance for practical MMN design. Numerical results validate the theoretical analysis of the achievable-rate upper bound and the effectiveness of the proposed MMN circuit realization method.

</details>


### [234] [On-board AI-based Channel Estimation for LEO NTNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15127)
*Mahdi Abdollahpour,Bruno De Filippo,Carla Amatetti,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向非地面网络(NTN)星上实时推理的轻量化卷积信道估计器，相比SOTA AI模型参数效率提升27%，计算量降低约29倍，同时MSE性能更优。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及卫星(NTN)和星上实时推理(onboard inference)，但核心任务是信道估计(channel estimation)，而非遥感目标检测。轻量化方法(参数效率提升、FLOPs降低)对模型压缩有借鉴意义，但场景和任务与遥感在轨目标检测不直接相关。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但星上轻量化推理的思路和领域知识驱动的参数效率优化方法有一定参考价值，可作为交叉领域泛读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：信道估计(channel estimation)；场景：非地面网络(NTN)、低轨卫星(NGSO)星上实时推理；传感器类型：通信信号(非遥感光学/SAR/多光谱)；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 轻量化卷积神经网络(lightweight convolution-based channel estimator)，具体结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 利用领域知识(domain knowledge)进行参数效率优化，相比SOTA AI模型参数效率提升27%，相比传统方法FLOPs降低约29倍。具体轻量化手段(剪枝/量化/蒸馏等)摘要未说明。

Onboard deployability: 面向星上实时推理(onboard inference)，在NGSO功率预算约束下评估性能。参数量未给出具体数值，FLOPs降低约29倍，延迟、显存、能耗等摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：均方误差(MSE)、链路性能(end-to-end link performance)。数据集摘要未说明。

Experiments: 与已有算法对比，所提方法在MSE上更优；与SOTA AI模型相比参数效率提升27%；与传统方法相比FLOPs减少约29倍。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 任务为信道估计，与遥感目标检测无关；2. 未说明具体轻量化技术细节(剪枝/量化/蒸馏等)；3. 未给出参数量、延迟等具体部署指标；4. 未在遥感数据集上验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 利用领域知识(domain knowledge)指导轻量化设计的思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测中(如利用遥感目标几何先验、场景先验)；2. 星上实时推理的约束条件(功率预算、计算资源受限)与在轨遥感目标检测场景类似，其评估框架可参考；3. 参数效率提升和FLOPs降低的量化对比方式值得借鉴。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence(AI) methods have shown strong channel estimation performance in terrestrial networks, but they typically rely on substantial computational resources. As 6G moves toward a unified architecture that will include Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) from day 0, availability of large and power hungry computational resources shall not be taken for granted. At the same time, NTN propagation often exhibits high predictability, limited multipath richness and significant Doppler shifts, representing a specific channel estimation problem. In this work, we propose a lightweight convolution-based channel estimator designed specifically for NTN operation and real-time onboard inference. We evaluate its channel estimation accuracy under stringent NGSO power budgets and quantify the resulting end-to-end impact on link performance. We show the improvement in terms of Mean Squared Error (MSE) achieved by the proposed approach compared with established algorithms, demonstrating that efficient AI models can deliver robust performance even on power-constrained spaceborne nodes. In addition, the proposed design by exploiting the domain knowledge, improves parameter efficiency by $27\%$ compared with state-of-the-art AI models and requires approximately $29\times$ fewer floating-point operations than conventional methods while achieving superior MSE performance.

</details>


### [235] [Modular-CAPA-Based Communication Systems: Joint Activation and Beamforming Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15148)
*Mengyu Qian,Xidong Mu,Li You,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究模块化连续孔径阵列（CAPA）在多用户通信中的子孔径激活与波束赋形联合优化问题，提出基于分支定界的最优算法和低复杂度的空间带宽感知方案。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是通信系统中的模块化连续孔径阵列（CAPA）与波束赋形问题，属于无线通信领域，与遥感、卫星在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多用户通信中的子孔径激活与波束赋形联合优化；应用场景：无线通信系统；传感器类型：连续孔径阵列（CAPA）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明与遥感或卫星相关。

Model architecture: 模块化CAPA架构，支持部分子孔径（sub-CAPA）激活。摘要未说明神经网络模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文提出的低复杂度空间带宽感知方案属于通信信号处理层面的计算简化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：频谱效率（SE）。摘要未说明使用的数据集。

Experiments: 通过数值仿真验证：1）部分激活的模块化CAPA性能优于相邻CAPA激活方案；2）B&B算法在SE上优于所有基准方案；3）空间带宽感知方案在性能-复杂度之间取得良好折中。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层研究，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: A modular continuous aperture array (CAPA)-based multi-user communication system is investigated, where only a portion of the aperture, namely sub-CAPAs, is activated to serve users. The signal model for the proposed modular CAPA is first introduced. Based on this model, a spectral efficiency (SE) maximization problem is formulated to jointly optimize the sub-CAPA activation and beamforming, subject to constraints on the limited number of active sub-CAPAs and the total transmit power. To address the resulting mixed-integer optimization problem, a branch-and-bound (B&B)-based algorithm is first proposed for optimal sub-CAPA activation and beamforming design. After that, the spatial bandwidth of the modular CAPA under partial activation is analyzed. The analysis reveals that a modular CAPA with partial sub-CAPAs activated could achieve a maximum spatial bandwidth comparable to that of a conventional CAPA. Motivated by this insight, a low-complexity spatial bandwidth-aware sub-CAPA activation scheme is further proposed. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that i) modular CAPA architectures with partial activation can consistently achieve greater performance gains than adjacent CAPA activations; ii) the proposed B&B scheme outperforms all benchmark schemes in terms of SE; and iii) the proposed spatial bandwidth-aware scheme provides an attractive performance-complexity tradeoff compared with the proposed B&B-based algorithm.

</details>


### [236] [Fast-Fading Channel and Power Optimization of the Magnetic Inductive Cellular Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04737)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Yongbin Gao,Wenjun Yu,Dongming Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于电磁场理论的3D天线振动模型，用于分析车辆磁感应通信中的快衰落信道，并利用非合作博弈和多智能体Q-learning优化网络吞吐量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是地下磁感应通信（MI communication）中的信道建模与功率控制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"vehicle"和"mobile"，但这是指地下环境中的移动通信车辆，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测毫无关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。应用场景为地下长距离磁感应通信网络，传感器类型为MI天线，不涉及任何遥感图像或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明任何深度学习模型或目标检测网络结构。提出的是信道建模方法（3D天线振动模型、共轭伪分段函数）和基于Q-learning的功率控制算法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测数据集。评价指标为CDF、PDF、信道期望、中断概率、网络吞吐量等通信领域指标。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证了信道模型推导和基于Q-learning的功率控制算法，未涉及任何目标检测实验或对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信信道建模领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: The cellular network of magnetic Induction (MI) communication holds promise in long-distance underground environments. In the traditional MI communication, there is no fast-fading channel since the MI channel is treated as a quasi-static channel. However, for the vehicle (mobile) MI (VMI) communication, the unpredictable antenna vibration brings the remarkable fast-fading. As such fast-fading cannot be modeled by the central limit theorem, it differs radically from other wireless fast-fading channels. Unfortunately, few studies focus on this phenomenon. In this paper, using a novel space modeling based on the electromagnetic field theorem, we propose a 3-dimension model of the VMI antenna vibration. By proposing ``conjugate pseudo-piecewise functions'' and boundary $p(x)$ distribution, we derive the cumulative distribution function (CDF), probability density function (PDF) and the expectation of the VMI fast-fading channel. We also theoretically analyze the effects of the VMI fast-fading on the network throughput, including the VMI outage probability which can be ignored in the traditional MI channel study. We draw several intriguing conclusions different from those in wireless fast-fading studies. For instance, the fast-fading brings more uniformly distributed channel coefficients. Finally, we propose the power control algorithm using the non-cooperative game and multiagent Q-learning methods to optimize the throughput of the cellular VMI network. Simulations validate the derivation and the proposed algorithm.

</details>

### [237] [OvAi Focus: AI-based Multi-class Segmentation of Functional Ovaries and Adnexal Masses in Gynecological Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14179)
*Niccolò Tallone,Francesca Salis,Pio Raffaele Fina,Roberta Massobrio,Rosilari Bellacosa Marotti,Daniele Conti,Luca Fuso,Luca Mariani,Annamaria Ferrero,Alessandro Arena,Stefano Cosma,Dan Grisaru,Angelo Lacalandra,Renato Seracchioli,Marianna Roccio,Federica Gerace*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一个用于卵巢癌超声图像分割的AI医疗软件设备OvAi Focus，实现多类语义分割。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是医学影像（超声）领域的卵巢癌分割任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

<details>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像语义分割（multi-class semantic segmentation）；场景：妇科超声影像；传感器：超声（US）；目标类别：adnexal masses（附件肿块）、functional ovaries（功能性卵巢）、cystic/solid components（囊性/实性成分）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及是stand-alone AI software medical device。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且应用场景为医疗软件设备，非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多中心数据集，1,081名成年女性，来自意大利和以色列6个中心。评价指标：DICE scores（0.87 complete lesion, 0.85 cystic, 0.68 solid, 0.62 functional ovary）。

Experiments: 在heterogeneous acquisition settings上验证，结果与SOTA相当或更优。摘要未说明对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医学影像分割领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the deadliest gynecological malignancy; accurate and objective segmentation of adnexal masses and functional ovaries in ultrasound (US) remains challenging due to operator variability and morphological complexity. We present OvAi Focus (SynDiag s.r.l., Italy), a stand-alone AI software medical device that performs multi-class semantic segmentation of functional ovaries and adnexal masses, distinguishing cystic from solid components. The system was trained and independently validated on a multicenter dataset of 1,081 adult women from 6 centers across Italy and Israel. Segmentation achieved DICE scores of 0.87 (complete lesion), 0.85 (cystic), 0.68 (solid), and 0.62 (functional ovary), in line with or superior to state-of-the-art approaches across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

</details>


### [238] [A Hybrid Framework for Blood Vessel Morphology Classification: Discrete Geometry-based Tortuosity Feature Measurement, Information Gain-based Feature Selection, and Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14195)
*Yu Zhong,Jingzhi Guo,Luyao Li,Zehao Wang,Zhihui Yang,Yixin Lin,Weilun Fu,Yang Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于离散几何特征、信息增益特征选择和随机森林的颈内动脉形态分类框架，用于血管迂曲度的客观量化分级。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医学影像中的血管形态分类（颈内动脉迂曲度评估），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：血管迂曲度形态分类（二分类和三分类）；应用场景：临床脑血管评估与卒中风险评价；传感器类型：医学血管造影（CTA/MRA等，摘要未明确说明）；目标类别：颈内动脉C1段（straight, low-tortuosity, high-tortuosity）。

Model architecture: 离散几何特征提取 → 信息增益特征选择 → Random Forest分类器。特征包括TI, AC, TC, AC/AT, AT, TT等13个迂曲度特征缩减至6个。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：379条临床血管中心线（内部临床数据，非公开遥感数据集）；评价指标：Macro-F1（二分类0.9206，三分类0.8626）。

Experiments: 二分类（非严重vs严重迂曲）Macro-F1=0.9206；三分类（straight/low-tortuosity/high-tortuosity）Macro-F1=0.8626；基于RF特征重要性定义了Morphological Risk Index (MRI)。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术方法或实验设置。医学影像形态分类与遥感目标检测在数据模态、任务目标、部署约束上差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。特征选择（Information Gain）和随机森林分类器在遥感领域已有广泛应用，但本文方法并无创新性贡献可迁移至遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题。

Abstract: Subjective visual grading of blood vessel tortuosity relies heavily on clinical experience, while traditional distance-based indices often fail to adequately characterize three-dimensional spatial deformation. Because abnormal internal carotid artery morphology may be clinically relevant to cerebrovascular assessment and stroke-risk evaluation, objective and reproducible quantification of vascular tortuosity is of considerable importance. To address this limitation, we propose a mathematical framework for the morphological classification of the internal carotid artery (ICA-C1) segment. The framework integrates discrete geometric feature measurement, Information Gain-based feature selection, and Random Forest classification. An initial set of 13 tortuosity features is extracted from the corresponding 379 clinical vascular centerlines using discrete geometric methods and subsequently reduced to a six-feature subset consisting of $\mathcal{TI}$, $\mathcal{AC}$, $\mathcal{TC}$, $\mathcal{AC}/\mathcal{AT}$, $\mathcal{AT}$, and $\mathcal{TT}$. The framework is evaluated in two classification tasks. For binary classification of non-severe and severe tortuosity, the RF model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.9206. For ternary morphological grading into straight, low-tortuosity, and high-tortuosity groups, it achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.8626. The results indicate that elongation- and curvature-related features provide strong discriminatory information for basic screening, whereas torsion-related features contribute additional information for more detailed morphological classification. Based on the RF feature-importance values, we further define a Morphological Risk Index (MRI), which provides a direct numerical reference for vascular morphology and may facilitate more objective and consistent clinical assessment.

</details>


### [239] [FORCE-Interior: A Poisson Flow Generative Prior for Interior Tomography Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14320)
*Kang Chen,Wenjun Xia,Jianxu Wang,Mahmud Wasif Nafee,Ge Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FORCE-Interior，一种基于泊松流生成模型的内部断层重建框架，用于解决投影截断导致的ROI重建病态问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是医学CT（计算机断层扫描）中的interior tomography（内部断层重建），属于医学影像领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感方向完全无关。不涉及目标检测，也不涉及轻量化或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学CT内部断层重建（interior tomography）；应用场景：医学影像；传感器类型：CT扫描仪；目标类别：人体组织/解剖结构。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: FORCE-Interior：基于Poisson-flow生成模型的生成式重建框架，包含全FOV测量约束初始化（full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization）和逐步数据一致性约束（per-step data consistency）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向医学CT重建，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及结构重建质量（structural reconstruction quality）和感知重建质量（perceptual reconstruction quality），以及投影域一致性（projection-domain consistency）。

Experiments: 在两种更严重截断的ROI尺寸上取得了更好的结构和感知重建质量，在最大ROI尺寸上具有竞争力的结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。该论文是医学影像重建方法，不涉及检测任务，不涉及轻量化，不涉及遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一的间接联系是：生成式方法中的数据一致性约束思想（data consistency）在遥感图像重建或超分中可能有启发，但与目标检测轻量化无直接关系。

Abstract: Interior tomography reconstructs a region of interest (ROI) from truncated projection measurements. However, projection truncation makes the inverse problem severely ill-posed, leading to non-unique solutions, oversmoothing, and truncation-induced artifacts when conventional reconstruction methods are directly applied to interior tomography. Existing learning-based interior CT methods have shown promising performance, but their generalization across different truncation patterns, ROI sizes, and noise levels remains an important challenge. Meanwhile, current generative model-based reconstruction methods are primarily designed for non-interior tomography settings and do not directly address ROI-based projection truncation. Moreover, without sufficient data-consistency constraints, generative sampling may yield anatomically plausible but measurement-inconsistent structures. To address these challenges, we propose FORCE-Interior, a Poisson-flow generative reconstruction framework for interior tomography. FORCE-Interior combines a full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization with per-step data consistency for ROI-truncated measurements, anchoring generative sampling to the acquired measurements throughout reconstruction. Experiments show that FORCE-Interior achieves improved structural and perceptual reconstruction quality at the two more severely truncated ROI sizes, with competitive results at the largest, while maintaining projection-domain consistency.

</details>


### [240] [ViPSAM: Visual Prompting Medical Image Segmentation Using Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14328)
*San Lee,Nalee Kim,Jeong Il Yu,Hee Chul Park,Boah Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ViPSAM，一种基于视觉提示的跨模态分割框架，利用增强MRI辅助非增强CT的肝脏病灶分割，基于SAM模型进行参数高效微调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向质子治疗中的医学图像分割（肝脏病灶分割），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分割（lesion segmentation）；应用场景：质子治疗规划（proton therapy planning）；传感器类型：非增强CT（NCCT）和增强MRI（contrast-enhanced MRI）；目标类别：肝脏病灶（liver lesion）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于SAM（Segment Anything Model）构建，包含视觉提示编码器（visual prompt encoder）、视觉引导交叉注意力模块（visual-guided cross-attention module）、掩码解码器（mask decoder）。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到"parameter-efficient manner"进行掩码解码器适配，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。无FLOPs、参数量、延迟等部署相关信息。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且SAM模型本身参数量巨大，不适用于星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：质子治疗中获取的肝脏病灶NCCT数据（具体规模摘要未说明）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称（如Dice、IoU等）。

Experiments: 与U-Net和SAM-based方法对比，ViPSAM表现更优。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 基于SAM的模型参数量巨大，不适合边缘/星上部署；4. 仅针对医学肝脏病灶分割，领域迁移性差。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限。但"视觉提示（visual prompting）"和"跨模态特征融合（cross-modality fusion）"的思路在遥感领域多模态融合（如SAR+光学、高光谱+多光谱）中有潜在借鉴价值，不过与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: In proton therapy planning, respiratory-gated non-contrast CT (NCCT) is commonly used for lesion segmentation; however, accurate delineation remains challenging due to low lesion-to-background contrast. Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance, they often struggle with non-contrast image segmentation. Inspired by clinical practice, where contrast-enhanced MRI is referenced to delineate lesions on NCCT, we propose ViPSAM, a visual prompting framework that leverages complementary cross-modality information. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), ViPSAM introduces a visual prompt encoder to extract guidance features from contrast-enhanced images and a visual-guided cross-attention module to integrate non-contrast and contrast-enhanced features, thereby enhancing lesion-relevant representations in low-contrast regions. The mask decoder is further adapted in a parameter-efficient manner to utilize visual prompts effectively. We evaluate the proposed method on liver lesion segmentation using NCCT acquired for proton therapy. Experimental results demonstrate that ViPSAM outperforms representative U-Net- and SAM-based methods, indicating that cross-modality visual prompting enables more robust and accurate segmentation in non-contrast images.

</details>


### [241] [Domain Adaptation of Mismatched Proximal Denoiser for Plug-and-Play Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14894)
*Guixian Xu,Jinglai Li,Junqi Tang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出"近端失配"概念，分析PnP-PGD图像重建方法在去噪器域外部署时的收敛性，并提出基于近端匹配的自适应策略来改善域偏移下的重建质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图像重建（去模糊、超分辨率）中的PnP-PGD算法收敛性分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像重建（Gaussian deblurring, super-resolution）；应用场景：通用图像处理；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: PnP-PGD（Plug-and-play proximal gradient descent）框架，使用去噪器作为隐式先验；涉及两类去噪器家族：learned proximal networks 和 gradient-step denoisers。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：Gaussian deblurring 和 super-resolution；域偏移设置：substantial domain shift；摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明，在域偏移下，proximal matching adaptation 相比 MSE-based adaptation 显著提升重建质量，在few-shot场景下增益最大。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；4. 实验仅在图像重建任务上验证，未涉及检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但"proximal mismatch"概念和域自适应思想有一定理论启发：在遥感在轨场景中，星上模型可能面临训练域（地面数据）与部署域（在轨数据）之间的域偏移，类似地可研究检测器在域偏移下的收敛性保证。然而本文方法本身（PnP-PGD图像重建）与目标检测轻量化方向差异较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Plug-and-play proximal gradient descent (PnP-PGD) enables flexible image reconstruction by using denoisers as implicit priors. In practice, these denoisers are often deployed outside their training domains. Existing analyses establish convergence under structural assumptions on the deployed denoiser, such as requiring it to be a proximal map or a contraction. However, they do not measure how domain mismatch affects convergence of PnP-PGD. We define this effect as \emph{proximal mismatch}: the discrepancy between a deployed denoiser $\widehat{\mathsf D}$ and a target-domain reference map $\mathsf D_\star=\operatorname{prox}_{R_\star}$ associated with the underlying regularizer $R_\star$. Under this mismatch, each denoising update becomes an inexact proximal step for the target objective. We further derive a stationarity bound that decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$, with an additive term proportional to the average squared proximal mismatch. This result motivates adaptation via proximal matching rather than MSE-based adaptation alone. We study this approach with two established denoiser families: learned proximal networks and gradient-step denoisers. Experiments on Gaussian deblurring and super-resolution under substantial domain shift show that proximal matching adaptation improves reconstruction quality significantly over MSE-based adaptation, yielding the largest numerical gains in the few-shot regime.

</details>


### [242] [Deep Scene-Driven Ordering of Hadamard Basis for Single-Pixel Spectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15045)
*Brayan Monroy,Hans Garcia,Henry Arguello,Jorge Bacca*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种场景驱动的Hadamard基排序方法，用于改进单像素成像（SPI）中深度光学编码设计（DOCD）的光谱图像重建质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于光谱图像获取硬件（单像素成像+光学编码），属于计算成像/光学设计领域，不涉及遥感卫星在轨目标检测，也不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：光谱图像重建（spectral image reconstruction）；场景：单像素成像（SPI）系统；传感器类型：VIS和NIR光谱传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。提及端到端优化（end-to-end optimization）的深度光学编码设计（DOCD）框架，核心创新是场景驱动的Hadamard基排序（scene-driven ordering of Hadamard basis）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了光谱数据集（spectral datasets）进行仿真，以及真实测试台采集（real test-bed acquisitions）。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在仿真和真实采集实验上，所提方法相比固定设计（fixed designs）提升了VIS和NIR光谱图像质量。未与目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及目标检测任务；2. 完全不涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署；3. 方法针对的是成像硬件层面的光学编码优化，而非算法模型层面的加速压缩；4. 未讨论在轨/星上场景的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：如果未来研究方向涉及星上光谱成像传感器的光学编码设计，可参考其场景驱动的自适应采样思路。但对于"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"这一核心课题，本文无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Spectral images are highly valuable for various applications, including environmental monitoring and precision agriculture. However, the high cost of specialized sensors limits the wide use of this technology in numerous applications. Current alternatives to acquire high spatial-spectral resolution spectral images, like Single-Pixel Imaging (SPI) enhanced with Deep Optical Coding Design (DOCD), have limitations due to their non-feedback optical designs, leading to limited image quality, with optimal performance achieved only for the specific scenes used during training. This work reformulates the DOCD framework to handle the scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard basis within the SPI architecture for spectral imaging. Taking into account that SPI usually acquires hundreds of snapshots, our approach introduces a scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard matrix for flexible SPI modulation pattern selection based on scene characteristics in an end-to-end optimization. Simulations on spectral datasets and real test-bed acquisitions demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in improving the quality of VIS and NIR spectral images compared to fixed designs.

</details>


### [243] [ESAR: Event-Based Synthetic Aperture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15073)
*Harbir Antil,Daniel Blauvelt,David Sayre*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出将单目事件相机成像建模为合成孔径逆问题，通过正则化反演恢复静态地面域对数辐射场，在近天底事件数据上恢复大尺度空间结构。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然涉及"near-nadir"（近天底）和"Falcon Neuro event data"（卫星事件相机数据），但论文核心是事件相机成像模型与重建方法，不涉及目标检测，不涉及轻量化，不涉及在轨部署。事件相机在遥感中虽有潜力，但本文聚焦底层成像逆问题，而非检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测，不涉及轻量化，不涉及在轨部署。仅对事件相机遥感成像有兴趣时可泛读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：事件相机成像重建（event-based imaging reconstruction）；场景：近天底对地观测（near-nadir Earth observation）；传感器：事件相机（event camera）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，重建的是场景辐射场的大尺度空间结构。

Model architecture: 提出线性化模型 APθ = b + η，其中θ为静态地面域对数辐射场，P为几何投影映射，A为时间差分算子，b为事件计数。通过正则化反演（regularized inversion）恢复θ。摘要未说明具体网络结构或深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。正则化反演的计算开销通常较大，但本文未讨论实时性或星上可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：模拟数据（simulated data）和真实Falcon Neuro近天底事件数据（real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体定量指标，仅定性描述"recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure"。

Experiments: 与动态潜像方法（dynamic latent-image baselines）和学习型事件重建方法（learned event-reconstruction baselines）对比，本文方法能恢复连贯的大尺度空间结构，同时抑制细尺度纹理。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 不涉及目标检测，无法直接用于遥感目标检测任务；2. 不涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩方法；3. 仅定性评估，缺乏定量指标；4. 正则化反演的计算效率未讨论，可能不适合星上实时处理；5. 仅针对近天底运动场景，泛化性有限。

Ideas for my research: 1. 事件相机在遥感中的应用是一个新兴方向，但其成像模型与目标检测的接口尚未打通，本文的辐射场重建可作为事件数据预处理的前端，但需要额外设计检测头；2. 本文的合成孔径结构分析（synthetic-aperture structure）对理解事件相机在卫星运动下的成像几何有参考价值，但与本课题的轻量化目标检测核心方向距离较远；3. 不建议作为本课题的核心参考论文。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous polarity events when changes in log--radiance exceed a fixed contrast threshold, producing signed temporal contrast measurements rather than conventional image frames. We formulate monocular event-based imaging as a synthetic-aperture inverse problem for a static ground-domain log--radiance field $θ\in \mathbb{R}^{N_g}$. Instead of reconstructing a latent pixel-time volume $v \in \mathbb{R}^{N_pN_t}$, we impose the geometric relation $v=Pθ$, where $P$ maps the fixed scene into motion-dependent latent views. Aggregating events over finite time intervals gives the linearized model \[
  APθ= b+η, \] where $A$ is a temporal differencing operator, $b$ contains signed binned event counts, and $η$ represents measurement and modeling errors. This decomposition exposes a synthetic-aperture structure: under near-nadir motion, successive projections are approximately shifted views of a common scene, while the composite operator $AP$ remains ill-conditioned because it combines spatial averaging with temporal differencing. We therefore use regularized inversion to recover $θ$. Numerical experiments on simulated data and real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data show that the proposed $θ$-based formulation recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure, relative to dynamic latent-image and learned event-reconstruction baselines, while suppressing fine-scale texture.

</details>

### [244] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向自主无人机蜂群搜索救援任务的三级分层学习架构，整合了Hebbian神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习与元学习三种不同学习机制，并通过形式化契约提供安全、最优性等六类保证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于无人机蜂群（UAV swarms）的搜索救援（SAR）任务，虽然涉及无人机和自主决策，但完全不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image目标检测、SAR/高光谱/多光谱遥感数据、空间在轨智能等核心主题。也没有任何轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、实时推理、边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：搜索救援（Search and Rescue）中的自主蜂群决策与协调。场景：无人机蜂群。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：摘要未说明。不涉及遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆/小目标/旋转框检测等）。

Model architecture: 三级分层学习架构：底层（Reflexes）使用Hebbian神经可塑性进行个体自适应；中层（Skills）使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）结合图神经网络（GNN）和行为树（Behavior Trees）进行战术协调；高层（Reasoning）使用模型无关元学习（MAML）结合BDI推理和数字孪生进行战略决策。架构通过22个形式化契约组织在6个组件中。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。虽然涉及无人机蜂群，但讨论的是决策架构而非模型部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。仅提到五种SAR任务类型的constructive progress functions，但未给出具体实验设置。

Experiments: 摘要未提供实验对比结果或消融实验。主要贡献是理论形式化分析，证明了架构解决了现有分层RL的五项基本局限，并提供了六类形式化保证（安全性、预算正确性、最优性、活性、无饥饿、层级一致性）。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显的问题是：1）该工作完全是理论/形式化层面的架构设计，缺乏实验验证；2）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；3）未涉及任何模型效率或部署可行性分析。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1）多层级架构设计思想（底层快速响应+中层协调+高层决策）可类比于星上轻量检测模型的级联设计（快速筛选+精细检测+后处理）；2）形式化契约保证的思路可用于验证轻量化模型在星上部署时的可靠性边界。但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向差异过大。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [245] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HG-RAG框架，通过层级知识图谱的图遍历来增强大语言模型的检索增强生成能力，解决平面文档检索在层级/关系推理任务上的不足。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于自然语言处理领域的检索增强生成（RAG）技术，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：检索增强生成（RAG）中的层级知识图谱检索。场景：自然语言问答与推理。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: HG-RAG框架，核心是层级知识图谱上的图遍历检索管道，包括命名实体锚定、向上（父节点）、横向（关系邻居）、向下（子节点）的上下文扩展。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 三个不同规模（18-800节点）的知识图谱数据集；四种查询类型：local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, multi-hop；评价指标包括推理准确率和幻觉率。

Experiments: 与稠密检索基线对比，HG-RAG在层级推理、关系推理和多跳推理任务上持续优于基线，同时减少幻觉并保持局部一致性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感、检测或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接借鉴点。该论文属于NLP领域的RAG方法改进，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [246] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为IMEX的可解释预测建模方法，通过静态相关功率(PCS)和交互相关功率(PCI)两个指标识别重要特征及其交互作用，并在合成数据集上验证了特征选择能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于可解释预测建模(XAI)中的特征重要性分析和交互作用发现，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可解释预测建模（特征选择与交互分析）；应用场景：通用机器学习可解释性；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: IMEX框架，包含两个互补指标：Static Correlation Power (PCS)用于量化单个特征贡献，Interaction Correlation Power (PCI)用于捕捉特征间的非加性交互效应。支持高阶交互分析（>2阶特征子集）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 三个具有已知结构的合成数据集(synthetic datasets)；对比方法为INVASE；评价指标为特征级结构恢复能力。

Experiments: 在三个合成数据集上，将IMEX的PCS组件与INVASE进行对比实验，结果表明IMEX在输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系时，能够有效恢复相关特征级结构。

Limitations: 1. 仅在合成数据集上验证，缺乏真实遥感/视觉任务评估；2. 仅验证了PCS组件，PCI组件未实验验证；3. 未讨论计算效率、可扩展性及大规模数据适用性；4. 与遥感目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果未来研究方向涉及遥感模型可解释性（如解释检测模型为何漏检/误检小目标），IMEX的特征交互分析方法可能有一定参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心目标（压缩、加速、部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [247] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向基因调控网络的多智能体AI框架RegNetAgents，用于癌症基因组学中的调控候选基因识别，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是生物信息学/癌症基因组学领域的基因调控网络分析，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向没有任何关联。摘要中未出现任何遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化相关术语。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：基因调控网络中的调控候选基因识别（regulatory candidate identification）；应用场景：癌症基因组学（breast cancer, colorectal cancer）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多智能体框架（multi-agent framework），基于LangGraph DAG workflow实现，包含dual-network classification、cancer gene filtering、mode-of-action assignment等模块。通过Python API和Model Context Protocol (MCP) client访问。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该框架作为下游分析层运行在预计算调控网络上，不涉及边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TCGA-derived cancer networks、GREmLN single-cell regulatory networks、OncoKB annotations；评价指标：Stouffer Z-score、p-value、富集分析（enrichment analysis）；实验设置：11个乳腺癌（BRCA）和12个结直肠癌（COAD）focal genes。

Experiments: TCGA-derived candidates显著富集OncoKB注释的癌症基因（BRCA: Z=6.69, COAD: Z=6.95）；GREmLN-derived candidates也显著富集（BRCA: Z=5.51, COAD: Z=7.06）；所有p<0.0001；管家基因和非驱动对照基因集无富集，支持信号特异性。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型计算效率、推理速度等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向无关，无可借鉴点。多智能体框架的设计思路（multi-agent workflow）在方法论层面有泛化参考价值，但与遥感目标检测轻量化的核心问题（模型压缩、加速、边缘部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [248] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对话式地点识别（DlgPR），将语言引导的地理定位从静态单次检索转变为交互式对话推理过程，并构建了首个大规模对话基准DlgQuest-Cities。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于语言引导的地理定位（language-guided geo-localization），属于多模态检索与对话推理任务，不涉及遥感图像目标检测、卫星在轨处理或模型轻量化。虽然"geo-localization"与地理空间相关，但其输入为自然语言描述+图像，输出为地点识别，而非遥感场景下的目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言引导的地理定位/地点识别（language-guided geo-localization, place recognition）；应用场景：城市级地点检索（对话式交互）；传感器类型：普通光学图像（非遥感卫星图像）；目标类别：地点/场景级识别，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 跨模态多级检索器（cross-modal multi-level retriever）+ 智能提问器DQ-pilot；DQ-pilot采用课程学习训练：先在DQ-cities-20k子集上进行监督微调（SFT），再在更难的DQ-cities-10k子集上通过GRPO进行强化学习优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向通用地点检索任务，未考虑星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DlgQuest-Cities（首个大规模对话式地点识别基准），包含DQ-cities-20k（用于SFT）和DQ-cities-10k（用于强化学习）；评价指标：Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) 用于课程采样，Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) 用于衡量提问带来的检索提升。

Experiments: 实验表明基于推理的对话式方法显著优于基线方法。具体对比方法和数值结果摘要未详细说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 未讨论实时推理或边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能相关的点是：对话式交互中的"多级检索"思想或许可类比遥感目标检测中的"由粗到精"检测策略（coarse-to-fine detection），但关联性较弱。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [249] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-T1D，将强化学习与LLM结合用于1型糖尿病胰岛素泵控制，通过知识蒸馏将RL策略迁移到大语言模型，实现可解释的血糖控制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是1型糖尿病胰岛素泵控制，属于医疗健康领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或遥感场景理解。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：胰岛素泵控制（blood glucose control）；场景：1型糖尿病治疗；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: 基于LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B的大语言模型，通过知识蒸馏从强化学习专家系统中迁移知识。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），但蒸馏对象是RL策略到LLM，并非用于模型轻量化或边缘部署目的，而是为了可解释性。摘要未说明剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型为8B参数级别的LLM，显然不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator（糖尿病模拟器）；评价指标：Time in Range（血糖在目标范围内的时间百分比，73.5%）。

Experiments: 在UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上测试，LLM控制器达到73.5% Time in Range，超越RL专家系统，并具备形式化安全验证能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或目标检测内容。8B参数模型在资源受限平台上部署困难。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能借鉴的点是"知识蒸馏"这一技术概念，但本文蒸馏的是RL策略到LLM，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化中的知识蒸馏（大检测模型蒸馏到小检测模型）场景差异很大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [250] [Human AI Construction of Bayesian Networks for Operational Decision Support -- A Virtual Survey Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14141)
*Kumar Rahul,Shovan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）和AI智能体面板来构建贝叶斯信念网络（BBN）的新方法，应用于医疗决策场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于贝叶斯网络构建与医疗决策分析领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：贝叶斯信念网络（BBN）的结构学习与参数估计；场景：替代医疗系统中患者就医意向建模；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: BBN框架，使用LLM驱动的AI智能体面板生成概率估计，结合trimmed-mean规则去噪。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。应用场景为医疗决策（customer intention to consult a doctor）。

Experiments: 通过案例研究展示了模型在医疗决策场景中的因果分析结果：自我效能（self efficacy）因果影响小，主观规范（subjective norms）影响更强。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于贝叶斯网络与LLM结合的概率建模方向，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Bayesian Belief Networks (BBNs) are powerful tools for decision-making under uncertainty. However, building their structures and estimating parameters are difficult. Currently, researchers must choose between relying on expert judgement or using large datasets to learn the structure and parameters of the network. We propose a new methodology using Large Language Models to bridge the gap between expert opinion and data-driven learning. This approach uses a panel of AI agents to estimate probabilities based on specific personas and context. We then apply a trimmed-mean rule to remove noise from these responses. We develop a six step BBN framework and illustrate it to model customer intention to consult a doctor in an alternative healthcare system. The model reveals that while self efficacy appears to be a major factor, its actual causal impact is small. In contrast, subjective norms have a much stronger effect in modelling customers' intention. The most effective strategy is to improve both confidence and community norms simultaneously.

</details>


### [251] [Capability from Access Structure, Not Scale: Lower Bounds and Pre-Registered Tests for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14144)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出能力收敛假说(CCH)，认为在固定推理预算下，不同架构的能力不会收敛于同一表示，而是收敛于"访问-完整混合体"——同时具备压缩O(1)状态通道和可扩展逐字索引通道的架构，并通过信息论下界和预注册实验验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯理论性的机器学习/信息论研究，讨论的是通用神经网络架构的表示收敛与能力收敛问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是通用神经网络架构理论，任务为"牛顿苹果问题"（无限流中的状态追踪），不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 提出"访问-完整混合体"(access-complete hybrid)架构概念，包含压缩O(1)状态通道和可扩展逐字索引通道。讨论了固定深度纯注意力架构、o(Nb)状态架构、固定窗口架构等通用架构类型，但未给出具体网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是"固定每token推理预算"下的能力收敛理论，但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等具体轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论研究，未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用预注册实验和合成数据（牛顿苹果问题），不涉及遥感数据集或标准评价指标。

Experiments: 报告了预注册小规模测试结果：剪刀差间隙被测量到（精确检索误差0.994 vs 0.000，当64标量状态获得一个全局注意力层时），状态追踪分叉落在注册边界上，联合见证显示不可约的双通道解；有一个预测失败且方向反转。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 纯理论工作，无实际部署考量；3. 实验规模极小（小规模测试）；4. 部分预测失败；5. 理论假设（可分离性假设）在真实遥感场景中是否成立未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发是：论文提出的"固定预算下不同架构能力收敛于混合体"的理论视角，或许可以启发思考在星上严格资源约束下，是否需要混合架构（如CNN+Transformer混合）来同时满足压缩表示和精确检索的需求。但这一联系非常间接，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that as models scale, representations of heterogeneous networks converge toward a shared model of reality. We propose its sequel and boundary, the Capability Convergence Hypothesis (CCH): under a fixed per-token inference budget, representational convergence does not entail capability convergence. Capability instead converges toward a class, the access-complete hybrid: any architecture holding both a compressive O(1)-state channel and a scalable verbatim-index channel. We anchor it on a witness task, the Newton's-apple problem in an infinite stream, and name three resource walls: a Shannon wall barring any o(Nb)-state architecture, a horizon wall barring any fixed window, and a circuit wall barring fixed-depth attention-only composition (conditional on TC0 != NC1). Under an explicit separability assumption a hybrid crosses all three by paying each wall's price, so capability is strictly super-additive under composition. We separate what we prove from what we conjecture: the access-completeness principle rests on information-theoretic lower bounds and pre-registered experiments, while the field-level convergence trend is an economics-motivated conjecture. We report the first pre-registered small-scale tests under criteria frozen before the data: the predicted scissors gap is measured (exact-retrieval error 0.994 vs. 0.000 once a 64-scalar state gains one global-attention layer), the state-tracking bifurcation lands at the registered boundary, and a conjunction witness shows an irreducibly two-channel solution; one prediction failed with its direction reversed and is reported as such. Representational convergence is given freely by scale; capability convergence must be purchased by access structure.

</details>


### [252] [ToolAnchor: Anchoring Counterfactual Context to Boost Agentic Tool-use Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14145)
*Weiting Liu,Jieyi Bi,Wanqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Yining Ma,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToolAnchor框架，通过反事实锚点上下文注入打破大语言模型智能体在工具集扩展时的行为惯性，实现动态工具适应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型智能体的工具集动态扩展问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型智能体工具集扩展（toolset expansion）；应用场景：通用AI助手（GAIA）、文本搜索（BrowseComp）、视觉搜索（VDR-Bench）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体架构，使用teacher-student框架进行反事实上下文生成与验证。具体模型名称摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或任何边缘/星上部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评估任务：GAIA（通用AI助手）、BrowseComp（文本搜索）、VDR-Bench（视觉搜索）。具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在GAIA、BrowseComp、VDR-Bench三个任务上评估，ToolAnchor在扩展工具集下表现出competitive performance。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或轻量化技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。论文讨论的是LLM智能体的工具适应问题，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络设计、边缘部署等）无直接关联。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents excel at long-horizon tasks, yet they are typically post-trained on fixed toolsets. When tasks demand new tools, these agents struggle to incorporate them effectively, and retraining from scratch is often impractical. We identify the core obstacle in such toolset expansion problem as behavioral inertia: the tendency of agents to fall back on familiar tools and established reasoning patterns despite having access to new ones. We demonstrate that injecting counterfactual anchor contexts at critical decision points can break this inertia, recovering failed trajectories by eliciting suppressed agent capabilities. To scale this insight, we propose ToolAnchor, a framework that uses teacher models to hypothesize these counterfactual contexts, verifies them via student rollouts, and internalizes the successful interventions through agentic post-training. Extensive evaluations across general AI assistant (GAIA), textual search (BrowseComp), and visual search (VDR-Bench) tasks demonstrate that ToolAnchor consistently exhibits competitive performance under expanded toolsets. Our work bridges the gap between static post-training and dynamic adaptation, charting a new path for scalable agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [253] [Enhancing Small Language Models Reasoning through Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14149)
*Dimitrios Kelesis,Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号代理框架，利用工具调用（extract_facts和get_hint）增强小语言模型（SLM）在多跳逻辑推理任务上的能力，在CLUTRR基准上测试Gemma 3和Llama 3.2。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于小语言模型（SLM）的推理增强，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）。虽然涉及"small language models"和"low-resource"，但讨论的是自然语言推理而非视觉模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自然语言多跳逻辑推理（kinship reasoning）；应用场景：CLUTRR亲属关系推理基准；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 神经符号代理框架：SLM（Gemma 3 1B/4B, Llama 3.2 3B）作为agent，调用两个工具——extract_facts（符号三元组提取）和get_hint（通过RGCN进行专家推理）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。虽然使用了"small language models"，但这是模型规模选择而非轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CLUTRR（kinship reasoning benchmark）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，但提及"1.5-2x performance gain"。

Experiments: 两种配置：Oracle场景（使用真实三元组）和Realistic场景（依赖自提取知识）。RGCN提示相比仅故事基线有1.5-2倍性能提升。发现提取瓶颈和顺序演绎脆弱性问题，以及特定架构中的"distraction effect"。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何视觉任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化技术；4. 不涉及边缘/星上部署；5. 实验仅在自然语言推理基准上进行。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发是：神经符号方法在资源受限场景下提升小模型能力的思路，但该论文面向NLP而非视觉，且不涉及遥感目标检测的具体问题（如小目标、旋转框、SAR图像等）。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have set benchmarks for zero-shot reasoning, their deployment remains cost-prohibitive and environmentally taxing. Small Language Models (SLMs) offer a sustainable alternative, but prone to errors, on tasks requiring complex, multi-hop logical grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic agentic framework to enhance the reasoning capabilities of SLMs, specifically Gemma 3 (1B, 4B) and Llama 3.2 (3B), using the CLUTRR kinship benchmark. Our approach transforms the SLM into a minimalist agent utilizing two specialized tool calls: extract_facts for symbolic triplet extraction and get_hint for expert reasoning via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (RGCN). We evaluate these models across two configurations, both in an Oracle scenario with ground-truth triplets and a Realistic scenario relying on self-extracted knowledge. Our results reveal that while RGCN-derived hints provide a 1.5 - 2x performance gain over story-only baselines, the system is constrained by the extraction bottleneck and sequential deductive fragility, where early extraction errors compound over multi-hop chains. Furthermore, we identify a "distraction effect" in specific architectures where noisy, self-generated facts degrade performance despite the presence of expert hints. This work characterizes the challenges of symbolic grounding in low-resource agentic systems and provides a roadmap for iterative verification in neuro-symbolic agentic pipelines.

</details>

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [255] [MemoHarness: Agent Harnesses That Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14159)
*Yue Huang,Wenjie Wang,Han Bao,Yuchen Ma,Xiaonan Luo,Yi Nian,Haomin Zhuang,Zheyuan Liu,Yue Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemoHarness，一种通过执行经验自适应优化LLM Agent控制层（harness）的框架，将harness分解为六个可编辑维度，利用双层经验库进行案例级自适应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM Agent的harness（控制层）优化，属于自然语言处理/智能体领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM Agent harness自适应优化；应用场景：shell-agent、代码生成、分析推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MemoHarness框架，将harness分解为context、tools、orchestration、memory、decoding、output handling六个可编辑控制维度；采用双层经验库（per-case diagnoses + distilled global patterns）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"additional context can remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable"，但这是针对LLM推理成本，而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估基准：shell-agent、code-generation、analytical-reasoning benchmarks；指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: MemoHarness相比固定harness有改进，并展示了向未见过的套件和基座模型的选择性迁移能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。论文自身也承认关于统计鲁棒性和组件归因的广泛声明留待未来工作。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM Agent领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: An agent harness is the external control layer that turns a base LLM into an executable agent by managing context, tools, orchestration, memory, decoding, and output handling. While harness design strongly affects agent behavior, most automatic improvement methods optimize narrower artifacts such as prompts, pipelines, or workflows, and deployed agents usually reuse a single global harness for all cases. We introduce MemoHarness, an adaptive harness optimization framework that learns from its own executions. MemoHarness decomposes the harness into six editable control dimensions, stores per-case diagnoses and distilled global patterns in a dual-layer experience bank, and adapts the learned harness to each test case using retrieved experience without test-time labels, feedback, or additional search. In our evaluation across shell-agent, code-generation, and analytical-reasoning benchmarks, MemoHarness improves over the fixed harnesses we compare against and shows selective transfer to unseen suites and base models. Its additional context can also remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable. These results provide evidence that execution experience is a practical substrate for building agent harnesses that are more adaptive than a single static configuration, while leaving broader claims about statistical robustness and component attribution to future work.

</details>


### [256] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了LLM合成的代码世界模型（CWM）即使通过高精度采样验证，仍可能在规划任务中系统性失败，原因是其遗漏了关键但稀有的动态规则，并提出了"验证-正确差距"的概念及量化规律。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM合成世界模型用于游戏规划的理论问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：游戏规则合成与规划（Code World Model）；应用场景：游戏AI；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: LLM（GPT系列）合成可执行代码作为世界模型，结合经典规划器进行搜索。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：游戏环境（具体游戏名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：transition accuracy（转移准确率）、state accuracy（状态准确率）、play cost（游戏代价）、rarity（稀有度）；样本量n=4800。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) CWM在采样门控上达到100%转移准确率和≥98%状态准确率，但在实际游戏中系统性失败；(2) 提出了danger = play_cost × (1 - rarity)^N的量化规律；(3) 增加数据（包括DAgger和针对性样本）无法修复该问题；(4) 在不完美信息CWM中证明了覆盖界理论。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身聚焦于游戏规划中的验证-正确差距理论问题，不涉及视觉感知任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但有一个方法论上的间接启发：论文提出的"验证-正确差距"概念——即高精度采样验证不能保证实际部署性能——对遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化有警示意义。在星上部署轻量化检测模型时，地面测试的高mAP可能掩盖在轨实际场景中的关键失败模式（如特定光照、特定目标姿态下的漏检），需要关注搜索分布（即实际部署分布）上的评估而非仅采样分布上的指标。

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning.
  We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples). (4) The same mechanism recurs on the belief-inference function of imperfect-information CWMs: we prove a coverage bound (a size-$N$ gate is identifying when $N\gtrsim b^{d_{\max}}$), explaining why shallow games such as Kuhn poker show no gap, and hand-construct Beacon, a verified-but-wrong inference function that passes the gate yet loses every game.
  These results suggest adequacy for planning-oriented world models should be measured on the search distribution or by play directly, not by prediction accuracy on sampled transitions.

</details>


### [257] [ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178)
*Yutong He,Daibo Li,Guohong Li,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang,Can Ren,Zekun Zhang,Yifan Liu,Shuchen Zhu,Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Ming Sun,Shu Li,Chenyi Li,Jiang Hu,Kun Yuan,Zaiwen Wen,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向理论驱动型科学发现的端到端自主智能体系统ReasFlow，用于自动生成包含严格推理和实验的研究论文。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于AI for Science / 大语言模型智能体方向，不涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：科学发现自主智能体系统；应用场景：理论驱动型科学研究（数学、定理证明、算法设计等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: ReasFlow系统包含：(i) 内部验证循环（internal verification loop），用于审计逻辑一致性并纠正错误；(ii) 自动化知识检索与自我改进机制（automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism）。系统统一了文献综合、算法设计、定理证明、实验和论文撰写。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。本文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评估方式：基于LLM的评审标准（LLM-based review rubric），与现有开源基线对比。未使用遥感或目标检测相关数据集。

Experiments: 系统自主生成了5篇完整研究论文，在LLM评审标准下取得最高评分。未涉及目标检测或遥感实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。摘要未说明推理效率、模型大小等与部署相关的信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM智能体方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无技术交集。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models have fueled autonomous AI agents capable of tackling complex scientific tasks, yet existing automated research systems remain predominantly focused on empirically driven domains with quantitative benchmarks, leaving theory-driven discovery, particularly in mathematically grounded disciplines requiring rigorous proofs and synthesis of domain knowledge, largely underexplored. Key challenges include the difficulty of verifying theoretical reasoning at scale, insufficient reasoning ability for autonomous frontier exploration, and a scarcity of procedural heuristics in the literature. We introduce ReasFlow, an end-to-end autonomous agent system for reasoning-centric scientific discovery that operationalizes a collaborative paradigm where the human expert acts as Principal Investigator while the agent executes rigorous derivations as a capable graduate student. ReasFlow incorporates (i) a robust internal verification loop that audits logical coherence and corrects fundamental errors prior to human inspection, and (ii) an automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism that proactively surfaces both declarative facts and overlooked procedural heuristics, substantially reducing expert intervention. The system unifies literature synthesis, algorithm design, theorem proving, experimentation, and manuscript preparation in a single system. Deployed to autonomously generate five complete research papers with rigorous theoretical and empirical content from minimal prompts, ReasFlow consistently achieves the highest evaluation scores among state-of-the-art open-access baselines under a curated LLM-based review rubric. ReasFlow is publicly accessible via the ReasLab platform, providing a collaborative workspace for AI-assisted theoretical research. Github repo: https://github.com/ReasLab/ReasFlow.git.

</details>


### [258] [RxBrain: Embodied Cognition Foundation Model with Joint Language-Visual Reasoning and Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14187)
*Haotian Liang,Mingkang Chen,Yufei Huang,Yuchun Guo,Xiaomeng Zhu,Xiangli Shi,Kaixuan Wang,Yunxuan Mao,Weijie Zhou,Ling Chen,Shirong Zeng,Yueyu Long,Yuchen Si,Yajuan Zhu,Xingyu Zhou,Minghui Wang,Wanjia He,Xin Yang,Lingzhu Xiang,Zhiqing Liu,Bohan Ma,Xiran Huang,Tianshuo Yang,Zhiheng Liu,Xuantang Xiong,Zisheng Lu,Ping Luo,Yao Mu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向具身认知的多模态基础模型RxBrain，通过语言-视觉联合推理与想象实现任务规划与物理状态预测，并扩展到机器人连续动作生成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身认知(embodied cognition)、机器人任务规划与视觉想象，属于机器人/具身AI领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身认知任务规划、语言-视觉联合推理、机器人动作生成。应用场景：机器人操作、具身AI。传感器类型：RGB摄像头（从embodied videos推断）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 统一多模态Mixture-of-Transformers架构，支持语言、图像、视频的理解与生成。将具身规划表示为单一规划序列，语言提供抽象结构（任务分解、规划原语、约束、时序、决策逻辑），视觉想象通过世界状态预测和联合子目标规划来具身化该结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型为大规模基础模型(Mixture-of-Transformers)，不面向边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 自建RxBrain-Bench评估数据集，用于评估模型是否能够通过联合文本和视觉组件表示具身规划。摘要未说明具体数据集规模、来源。评价指标：具身理解与生成能力、规划质量（文本推理、世界状态预测、联合子目标规划），以及连续机器人动作生成的实机表现。

Experiments: 实验表明RxBrain保持了具身理解和生成能力，能生成耦合文本推理、世界状态预测和联合子目标规划的规划序列。在连续机器人动作生成任务上展示了有前景的实机表现，无需大规模动作数据预训练。摘要未提供与基线方法的定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 模型规模大（Mixture-of-Transformers），不适用于资源受限的星上部署场景。3. 摘要未提供定量指标（mAP、FPS、参数量等），缺乏可复现的对比基准。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1) Mixture-of-Transformers架构思路可关注，但需大幅轻量化才能用于星上；2) 语言-视觉联合推理范式与遥感场景中"语言引导的检测"或"视觉-语义联合理解"有概念上的相似性，但需重新设计。总体而言，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Embodied cognition requires agents to connect high-level task reasoning with the physical states to be achieved. We introduce Hy-Embodied-RxBrain, an embodied cognition foundation model with joint language-visual reasoning and imagination. Unlike vision-language models that emphasize scene understanding and textual decision making, or generative world models that mainly predict future visual states, RxBrain represents embodied plans in a single planning sequence where language and visual imagination play complementary roles. Language provides the abstract structure of a plan, including task decomposition, planning primitives, constraints, temporal order, and decision logic, while visual imagination grounds this structure through world state prediction and joint subgoal planning, associating each planning step with intermediate and final physical states. RxBrain adopts a unified multimodal Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports language, image, and video understanding and generation within one model. To train this capability, we build an automatic pipeline that converts embodied videos into joint text-visual planning supervision by decomposing videos into planning steps and aligning them with visual state transitions. We further introduce RxBrain-Bench to evaluate whether models can represent embodied plans through joint textual and visual components rather than separate understanding or generation. Experiments show that RxBrain maintains embodied understanding and generation abilities, and produces plans with coupled textual reasoning, world state prediction, and joint subgoal planning. We also extend RxBrain to continuous robot action generation, where it shows promising real-robot performance without large-scale action-data pretraining. These results provide an initial step toward foundation models for embodied cognition.

</details>


### [259] [How Artificial Intelligence LLM Engines Shape the Global Conflict Information Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14197)
*Jason Miklian*

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。论文研究的是LLM回答冲突问题的准确性，属于自然语言处理和社会科学交叉领域。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及了多个AI answer engines和LLM，但未给出具体模型名称或结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 论文对28个冲突问题向5个answer engines提问，收集了5,460个答案，并基于文献证据进行评分。分析了1,048个网站来源。但未涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 主要发现：冲突信息记录越薄弱，引擎越容易编造、错误归因和错误计数；薄记录不仅助长幻觉，还容易受到GEO操纵；国家派系的数字信息捕获虽处于初期但增长迅速。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，没有任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/社会科学交叉研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术关联。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) answer engines now field a growing share of the questions that analysts, scholars, and the public ask about issues of peace and conflict. Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to hallucinate under certain conditions, but do these errors have discernible patterns when they are asked about conflicts, and if so what can that teach us about the changing global conflict information environment? To answer, we first asked a battery of questions about 28 conflicts to five leading answer engines and scored their 5,460 answers against documented evidence. We found that the thinner the retrievable record around a given conflict, the more the engines invent, misattribute, and miscount. Thin records don't just encourage hallucination, but create structural exposure to mis- and disinformation, because they are the easiest records to warp through Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to bias engine responses. Through an analysis of 1,048 websites that the AI LLMs pulled conflict facts from, we found that GEO source optimization is already happening, and while state-partisan digital capture remains incipient it is rapidly growing. We explain what these findings mean for scholarship with the rise of GEO information warfare, and for policy argue for a return to the deep local monitoring and translation-based research that AI tools cannot replicate, closing with a discussion of future research opportunities and challenges in this fast-moving space.

</details>


### [260] [Align AI to Dynamic Human-AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14240)
*Valerie Chen,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Anita Williams Woolley,Michael Lee,Tongshuang Wu,Vincent Conitzer,Aarti Singh*

TL;DR: 论文主张将AI对齐从静态模仿人类偏好转向交互式、互补的对齐范式，强调偏好通过交互动态涌现，而非静态满足。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于AI对齐（AI alignment）与人类-AI交互的理论性研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文讨论的是AI对齐的哲学与社会科学视角，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化的技术路线（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络设计、边缘部署等）无任何交集。

Abstract: Current alignment approaches typically focus on emulating human behavior using static representations of human preferences, failing to capture the dynamic, context-dependent nature of real-world human-AI interactions. In this paper, we argue for a shift from static and emulative to interactive and complementary alignment, where preferences emerge through interaction and alignment is defined not by satisfying preferences alone. We first formalize this gap by contrasting existing alignment with a trajectory-level view in which human and model behavior co-evolve over time. Because these interaction dynamics have not been adequately captured within existing ML formulations, we ground this perspective in insights from an interdisciplinary workshop. We draw on lessons from social-science accounts of human-human collaboration and then argue that human-AI systems amplify these dynamics, introducing new asymmetries that make reasoning about uncertainty harder and introduce new coordination challenges. Based on these lessons and new challenges, we conclude by outlining a research agenda for developing AI systems that align with humans in interaction, requiring an interdisciplinary synthesis of machine learning and the social and decision sciences.

</details>


### [261] [The Steering Budget: Examples beat Knobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14246)
*Raj Kumar Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型中"旋钮"(prompt/guidance)与"示例"两种控制方式对属性调控范围的差异，提出训练数据中存在一个"预算"决定了属性可移动范围，示例可以覆盖旋钮无法触及的部分。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是生成模型(图像生成和晶体结构生成)的控制机制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文聚焦生成模型的可控性问题，而非判别式检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是生成模型的控制机制，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Generative models are steered with knobs -- prompts, guidance scales, property tags. Turn one as hard as you like and, past a point, it stops moving the property you care about. We find that ceiling is not a shortcoming of the model but a budget, set by the training data before the model is trained: a property's movable range splits in two -- the part a knob can reach, and a second, significant part that only examples -- concrete instances of what you want more of -- can reach. That second part is usually much larger, but not always, and the same budget says so in advance.
  Reaching that second part takes a different move: instead of turning a knob, you show the model examples, composed from what it already learned rather than added to its training. A cheap audit of the training data measures the budget; we give a recipe for building the example set that reaches all of it.
  This buys two things a knob can't. Reach: it moves a property across the whole budget, not just the part a knob reaches. Expressiveness: it steers toward targets you can only specify by example -- including ones you can't put into words. We turn these into a handful of falsifiable claims and verify them in two unrelated domains, image and crystal-structure generation -- marking where a knob is enough, and where only examples will do.

</details>


### [262] [Automatic Hard Example Synthesis with Multi-Level Agentic Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14256)
*Genglin Liu,Muye Zhang,Krishnamurthy Viswanathan,Nichole J. Hansen,Blaž Bratanič,Nathan L Clement,Shalini Ghosh,Ariel Fuxman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多智能体架构的自动化红队测试框架，通过迭代生成对抗性多模态示例来提升大语言模型的内容安全鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的内容安全与红队测试，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。既不是遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及任何模型压缩或边缘部署技术。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的是"多智能体协作"或"test-time Retrieval"的范式，但与本课题的遥感目标检测轻量化方向差距过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed for nuanced content safety and moderation tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks and out-of-distribution edge cases. Traditional active learning and manual annotation fail to scale against the complexity and volume of novel multimodal threats. In this paper, we propose an automated, agentic red-teaming framework that systematically synthesizes difficult examples using an iterative strategy that proposes novel hypotheses as well as mutating on past attempts. Leveraging a multi-agent architecture that consists of a high-reasoning Architect agent, an advanced image generator, and a multi-level verification committee of LLM raters, our system autonomously uncovers boundary-pushing violations and ambiguous policy edge cases without any human intervention. By employing these carefully synthesized adversarial examples as in-context demonstrations via test-time Retrieval, we substantially improve the target model's robustness, reducing the False Negative Rate (FNR) from 41.2% to 24.5% in a public image safety benchmark without relying on any human labeling.

</details>


### [263] [AI Agents Do Not Fail Alone:The Context Fails First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14275)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文聚焦于AI Agent的上下文工程（context engineering）质量评估，讨论的是LLM Agent的指令遵循、工具使用、幻觉抵抗等行为可靠性问题。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。
