Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医疗AI安全评估领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、技术方法、评价体系上均无交叉。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [317] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养测试中的表现，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。研究主题是科学可视化（SciVis）素养评估，属于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在可视化理解方面的基准测试，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为科学可视化素养评估，涉及8种可视化技术和11种任务类型（如搜索、空间理解、定量估计等），传感器类型和目标类别均不适用。

Model architecture: 评估了6种MLLM，包括3种闭源模型和3种开源模型（具体模型名称摘要未完全列出，仅提及Gemini表现最强）。不涉及目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用科学可视化素养评估测试（SciVis literacy assessment test），包含49个测试项、18个科学可视化图表，覆盖8种技术和11种任务类型。对比了485名人类参与者的数据。评价指标为模型在该测试上的正确率。

Experiments: Gemini在评估子集上超过人类平均水平，开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，在基于纹理和集成的可视化以及定量估计任务上表现较差。错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解释和接地编码解释方面的重复失败。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是MLLM在科学可视化理解上的能力评估，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接或间接参考价值。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [318] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI基准测试中项目反应理论(IRT)建模的可靠性问题，探讨在LLM基准场景下不同IRT估计工具的可行性、可扩展性和推断可靠性，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于AI基准测试中的项目反应理论(IRT)建模方法，讨论的是LLM评估的统计方法论问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是IRT统计模型（marginal maximum likelihood, MCMC, variational inference, neural pseudo-Siamese estimator），而非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了六个广泛使用的LLM基准数据集，但摘要未说明具体名称。评价指标涉及计算可行性、可扩展性、模型排名推断可靠性、预测性能、项目特征推断可靠性等。

Experiments: 在18,000个模拟条件下，比较了四种IRT估计工具，评估了计算可行性、可扩展性和推断可靠性。结果表明经典估计器在大规模基准场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小规模或非正态分布的模型集上可能产生不可靠的推断。

Limitations: 论文本身与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。其研究场景（LLM基准测试的IRT建模）与本研究课题（遥感图像目标检测的轻量化部署）在问题域、方法论和技术栈上均无交集。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于AI评估的统计方法论研究，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题在技术路线、应用场景和核心问题上均不相关。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [319] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Plover，一种以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过外部化任务计划和重规划为可检查、可修改的工件，提升GUI自动化的透明性和可控性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是GUI自动化（图形用户界面自动化），属于人机交互与多模态智能体领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。没有任何遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化部署的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：GUI自动化（图形用户界面自动化）；应用场景：桌面/移动端界面操作；传感器类型：截图（screenshot）；目标类别：界面元素（按钮、对话框等）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Planner-Executor架构（规划器-执行器架构），外部化任务计划为可检查、可修改的工件。摘要未说明具体网络结构或视觉backbone。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 形成性研究（6名参与者）；基准故障案例修复评估；场景化工作流分析。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和量化指标。

Experiments: 通过基准故障修复和场景化工作流分析评估，结果表明GUI智能体故障在计划可见且干预局部化时可结构化修复，显式重规划有助于提升透明性、可控性和适应性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究GUI自动化，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [320] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向抑郁症数据集标注的LLM+专家协同框架，用于生成符合DSM-5-TR标准的可解释标注，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是心理健康领域的抑郁症数据集标注质量与可解释AI，属于自然语言处理+临床心理学交叉方向。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为抑郁症（MDD）数据集的可解释标注，场景为心理健康/临床文本分析，传感器类型不适用，目标类别不适用。

Model architecture: 提出三阶段标注框架：候选证据选择→DSM-5-TR标准分析→病例级综合标注。采用双记忆架构（Example Memory + Reflection Memory）来内化专家反馈。使用LLM辅助标注。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用抑郁症相关数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）。评价指标为标注一致性（annotation consistency）和人工修订工作量（manual revision effort）。

Experiments: 进行了专家评审的试点研究（pilot study），结果显示方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，减少了人工修订工作量。但摘要明确说明多轮反馈循环的评估留待未来工作。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术或思路。2. 该工作本身也处于早期阶段，多轮反馈评估尚未完成。3. 依赖LLM和专家介入，计算开销大，不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是临床文本标注，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [321] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中文本内容危险与物理世界危险的表征可分性，提出基于隐状态探测的PRISM方法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是大语言模型(LLM)的安全对齐问题，聚焦于文本内容危险(Content Danger)与物理世界危险(Physical Danger)的表征可分性。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法；不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM安全对齐、物理危险检测；应用场景：具身智能体安全；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: PRISM：基于全隐状态的单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针(probe)；基座模型：Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B、Phi-3.5、SmolLM2。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。PRISM本身是一个轻量探针（单层逻辑回归），但这不是针对遥感模型或检测模型的轻量化方法，而是LLM安全对齐中的探测方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SafeAgentBench、PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K，自建对比基准，1000个物理风险对)、SafeText、EARBench；评价指标：准确率(Accuracy)、假阳性率(FPR)。

Experiments: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，11.7-13.7% FPR；同规模LLM法官过阻安全任务达24.7-39.0% FPR。在PSB-1K上PRISM达99.6%准确率、0.7% FPR，而Qwen2.5-3B法官拒绝67.8%安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上复现结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。论文本身局限：仅验证了CD/PD在LLM表征中的可分性，未讨论物理危险检测在实际机器人系统中的部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM安全对齐领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [322] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个用于自动化元分析的多智能体系统AutoSynthesis，实现从文献检索到效应量合并的全流程自动化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于自动化元分析（meta-analysis）和证据合成（evidence synthesis），属于自然语言处理/科学文献挖掘领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动化元分析（automated meta-analysis）；场景：科学文献证据合成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多智能体系统（multi-agent system），端到端流水线，包含搜索策略制定、文献检索、研究筛选、全文评估、统计量提取、效应量计算、随机效应元分析等模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向科学文献分析，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：28篇研究文献；评价指标：与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g效应量一致性。

Experiments: 在28篇研究上进行了应用，提取了20余个定量结论，合并效应量与专家手动元分析结果接近。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、加速或部署方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于科学文献挖掘和自动化元分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [323] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统teLLMe，利用因果结构学习、LLM和DoWhy进行交通事件的因果效应估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦城市交通因果分析（causal analysis of urban driving datasets），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果分析（causal analysis）；应用场景：城市交通（urban driving）；传感器类型：dashcam（行车记录仪视频）；目标类别：交通事件（traffic events），非遥感目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 系统组件：PC算法（因果结构学习）、bootstrap稳定性检查、线性回归、DoWhy（因果推断库）、schema-aware LLM（大语言模型）用于自然语言到结构化因果查询的映射。不涉及任何目标检测或轻量化网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BDD（Berkeley DeepDrive）衍生的交通事件数据；评价指标：因果效应估计（effect estimates）、DAG支持度（DAG support）、调整集（adjustment sets），非目标检测指标（mAP、F1、IoU等）。

Experiments: 案例研究（case studies）展示系统可发现天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的合理因果关系，并显式呈现不确定性和建模选择。无目标检测对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。该论文是因果推断+LLM在交通领域的应用，不涉及任何视觉检测模型的设计、优化或部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [324] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SearchOS多智能体框架，通过显式状态管理解决大模型工具集成中的搜索循环和预算浪费问题，用于开放域信息检索。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的web搜索和信息检索，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：开放域信息检索(open-domain information seeking)；场景：web搜索、多智能体协作；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: SearchOS多智能体框架，包含Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM)、pipeline-parallel调度机制、Search Tool Middleware Harness、层级技能系统(strategy skills + access skills)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向云端LLM推理，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WideSearch、GISA；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称，仅称"leads all metrics"。

Experiments: 在WideSearch和GISA上，SearchOS在所有指标上优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该工作面向LLM+web search场景，属于NLP/信息检索领域。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。但多智能体协作中的"Failure Memory"（失败记忆）和"Coverage Map"（覆盖图）概念，在思路上可类比于遥感在轨检测中的"难例回传"或"检测失败场景记忆"机制，但属于非常间接的启发。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [325] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究的是通过公开讨论界面（如论坛、评论区）向语言模型预训练数据注入投毒攻击的方法，以及提出HalfLife分析方法来估计恶意内容是否被爬取并纳入训练数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自然语言处理（NLP）领域的语言模型预训练数据投毒攻击，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：预训练数据投毒攻击分析与检测；应用场景：语言模型（LM）预训练安全；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，讨论的是语言模型（LM）的预训练数据投毒问题，非视觉模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。提出HalfLife分析方法用于估计对抗性内容在基于网络爬取的LM训练数据中的包含情况。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP安全领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>

### [326] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出GNDR框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，实现不确定性量化，应用于临床计数、生存分析、面部图像年龄分布等任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于概率统计与深度学习的交叉方法研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：概率分布回归与不确定性量化；应用场景：临床计数、转录组生存分析、面部图像年龄估计；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，上游网络作为固定非线性基展开，终端预测头隔离处理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：临床计数数据、转录组生存数据、面部图像年龄数据；评价指标：分布校准（distributional calibration）、置信带（confidence bands）、容忍区间（tolerance intervals）。

Experiments: 在过离散临床计数、右删失转录组生存分析（混合治愈框架）、零截断面部图像年龄分布三种数据模态上验证了框架的通用性和优越的分布校准性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；未涉及任何检测任务或遥感场景；未讨论计算效率或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，其不确定性量化方法（Fisher信息矩阵、Delta方法）可能对遥感目标检测中的预测置信度评估有理论参考价值，但与本课题核心的轻量化、在轨部署方向不直接相关。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [327] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将算子条件高斯过程回归扩展到复值Helmholtz方程波场推断，通过实部-虚部解耦实现后验不确定性量化，应用于脑磁共振弹性成像。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是物理信息驱动的波动方程求解与不确定性量化，属于计算物理/声学/医学成像领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星平台或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：复值Helmholtz方程波场推断与不确定性量化；场景：耗散介质中的波动传播，脑磁共振弹性成像（MRE）；传感器类型：非遥感传感器；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 算子条件高斯过程回归（operator-informed GP regression），通过将复值算子实部-虚部解耦为等价的耦合实值块，使用标准实值GP条件化进行推断。支持对角先验、协同区域化先验和多尺度先验。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：一维到三维Helmholtz方程基准问题；应用数据：体内脑磁共振弹性成像（MRE）数据；评价指标：与测量值的相关性（correlation），目标阈值0.75，达到0.77。

Experiments: 与有限差分法和神经网络基线对比，在更少的内部约束点下具有竞争力；多尺度先验重建剪切旋度场相关性0.77，超过0.75目标；增益来自多尺度核而非实-虚耦合；识别了低频精度上限（模型失配导致）和后验不确定性未校准的问题。

Limitations: 摘要明确指出了两个局限性：1）存在由模型失配导致的低频精度上限；2）后验不确定性尚未校准（not yet calibrated），这是下一步的核心工作。此外，与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来研究涉及遥感物理模型（如SAR成像模型）与数据驱动方法的结合，该文的不确定性量化框架可能有参考意义，但与在轨目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联极低。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [328] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏随机几何图的谱性质与潜向量恢复，属于随机图论与高维统计领域，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1 分（基本无关）。论文主题是随机几何图的谱范数界、潜向量恢复和混合块模型精确恢复，属于理论计算机科学/高维概率/随机图论方向，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1 分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [329] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究最优自蒸馏方法用于修正rectified flow中的次优教师速度场，通过理论推导最优混合系数并证明可严格提升速度估计性能。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（self-distillation），但蒸馏目标是提升生成质量而非模型轻量化/压缩。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等轻量化方法。

Experiments: 实验验证了最优自蒸馏在velocity risk、mode recovery和finite-step generation方面优于教师模型和纯蒸馏方法。

Limitations: 1. 理论与实验均局限于生成模型领域，与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 未讨论任何遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化部署问题；3. 理论推导基于线性RF和ridge正则化假设，泛化到复杂非线性模型需进一步验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的连接点是：自蒸馏（self-distillation）作为一种知识蒸馏变体，理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化中，但本文聚焦生成模型而非检测模型，且理论假设（线性RF、ridge正则化）与检测模型差异较大，直接借鉴困难。

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>


### [330] [cGAP: Generalized Association Plots with HOMALS-Guided Heatmaps for Visualization of High-Dimensional Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15018)
*Chun-houh Chen,Shun-Chuan Chang,Chiun-How Kao,Yi-Ju Lee,Shang-Ying Shiu,Yin-Jing Tien,ShengLi Tzeng,Han-Ming Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出cGAP框架，用于高维分类数据的可视化，通过Homogeneity Analysis将分类数据嵌入三维空间并映射为RGB颜色，实现可解释的矩阵热图展示。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于高维分类数据可视化领域的方法论文。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感或目标检测相关任务。应用场景为遗传学、生物医学、社会科学中的分类数据分析。传感器类型和目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: cGAP框架包含三个协调视图：HOMALS引导的原始数据矩阵热图、主体邻近矩阵、变量邻近矩阵。使用Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS)进行嵌入，将分类数据映射到三维欧氏空间并转换为RGB坐标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了四个数据集：学生-动物分类数据、哺乳动物齿廓数据、UCI蘑菇数据集、直系同源基因簇数据库。评价指标为定性可视化效果，包括聚类、异常值检测、局部到全局结构揭示能力，以及理论性质（重心可追溯性、投影畸变、对比度保持）。

Experiments: 通过四个不同领域的分类数据集展示cGAP的可视化效果，证明其支持透明探索性分析，保持导出视觉结构与原始分类观测之间的可追溯性。摘要未提供定量对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化任何相关方向；2. 仅适用于分类数据可视化，不涉及检测或识别任务；3. 缺乏定量评价指标和与现有方法的量化对比；4. 未讨论计算效率或大规模数据可扩展性。

Ideas for my research: 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，其数据矩阵重排（seriation）思想或可用于遥感检测结果的后处理可视化排序，但价值极低。

Abstract: High-dimensional categorical data arise in genetics, biomedicine, and the social sciences, yet visualization tools for such data remain far less developed than those for continuous variables. Existing methods either scale poorly, rely heavily on low-dimensional displays detached from the original data matrix, or prioritize predictive accuracy over interpretability. To address this gap, we introduce categorical Generalized Association Plots (cGAP), a visualization framework for nominal, ordinal, and binary data that preserves the original data matrix while augmenting it with interpretable geometric structure. cGAP uses Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS) to embed subjects and category levels in a three-dimensional Euclidean space and maps the embedding to red-green-blue coordinates so that similar patterns receive similar colors. The framework integrates three coordinated views: a HOMALS-guided heatmap of the raw data matrix, a subject proximity matrix, and a variable proximity matrix. Seriation algorithms are then used to reorder rows and columns to reveal coherent clusters, outliers, and local-to-global structure. We also derive barycentric traceability, projection-distortion, and contrast-preservation properties that clarify how embedding geometry is transferred to the display. We demonstrate the versatility of cGAP through applications to student-animal classification data, mammalian dentition profiles, mushroom records from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, and the Clusters of Orthologous Genes database. These examples show that cGAP supports transparent exploratory analysis by maintaining traceability between derived visual structure and the original categorical observations. cGAP provides a full-matrix, heatmap-based visualization environment for investigating complex categorical datasets across scientific domains.

</details>


### [331] [Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15196)
*Raghad Alamri,Michele Caprio,Gavin Brown*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于主观风险分解的不确定性量化新视角，将认知不确定性和偶然不确定性视为高阶建模决策的衍生结果，而非原始概念。

Abstract: We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition. We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.

</details>
